# Camel Finance: extracted rules (consolidated)

Source slug `camel-finance`. School: **cycle**. Channel: Camel Finance ("Camel"), YouTube. **69 daily transcripts, 2026-07-25 to 2026-10-17.** The five part files listed below read 66 of them in full (14 + 17 + 11 + 12 + 12). I read the other three in full for this file: 2026-08-29 `48gjRRnmyY0`, 2026-09-08 `fDMJEhj2CGo` and 2026-09-25 `luWzbwZ02mg`, all Whisper transcripts.

I also read in full **nine older or education videos** that the parts do not cover. All but the 2023 one are YouTube ASR transcripts. Where one of their rules differs from 2026 practice, it is marked with its year.

- 2026-05-07 "The 4 Year Cycle is Dead - Metals to New Highs!" (`z_s9A8VEXGU`)

For the indicator section I also searched all of the transcripts, and I read the vendor's public documentation pages for the CF Cycle Trading Indicator: about 50 GitBook pages (current and the archived 1.0) plus the cftrading.co.uk sales page. They are cited by URL.

Material: daily live market calls with a "TA and live positions" segment, Saturday "deep dives" that re-check his base case, a pre-recorded Q&A (18 Aug, recorded 14 Aug), and teaching segments (28 Aug translation, 17 Sep long-term vs short-term accounts, 14 Oct BTC proxies). Access: public.

Not obtained:
- the 16 members-only ("Level 3") videos listed in the manifest;
- one public daily: 2026-10-16 "This Week Saw Panic - is it over?" (`l0Dg-rb1u98`, blocked by a YouTube bot check).

Level 3 members get exact entries and stops in real time, and they are almost never spoken on air. Positions can therefore be scored only at the level of thesis and invalidation, not at entry/stop level.

The transcripts are Whisper large-v3-turbo. ASR mangles are corrected in paraphrase only: "Bresset/Bressa/breast/brecet" = DSS Bressert, "Dixie" = DXY, "Siljay" = SILJ, "ball" = bull, "Campbell" = Camel.

Where the method changed, or where he stated a rule inconsistently over time, the versions are shown with dates. None of them has been silently picked. Only Camel's own rules are described here. Rules he quotes from other people (Bob Loukas, Ben Cowen, Green Star Trading, Erlovsky, Henrik and others) are marked as quoted.

## Method in its own terms

Camel trades **nested time cycles** ("Russian nesting dolls"). Daily cycles sit inside weekly cycles, which sit inside each asset's large cycle: Bitcoin and US stocks have a 4-year cycle, gold an 8-year cycle (its half-cycle low is the 4-year low), TLT a 7.5-year cycle and DXY a 3-year cycle. In his words, "cycles predict time, not price". The work is to find the high-probability low inside its **timing window** and manage risk around it. The window is the average low-to-low length, plus or minus 10%, and the 4-year low gets plus or minus one weekly cycle.

He waits for the window to open. Then he waits for the low to be **confirmed on that cycle's own timeframe**, by some mix of these: a swing low, a close back above the 10 SMA, a DSS Bressert bullish cross out of the bottom, a trendline break, a big green thrust candle, and his own CF cycle indicator "picking" the low. He would rather be late and confident than early. He prefers buying the first higher low after the breakout to buying the low itself. The stop goes a little under the cycle low and is sized so that it costs about 2% of the account. He adds at each later higher low.

**Translation** tells him the trend. A cycle that peaks after its midpoint is right-translated (bullish). A cycle that peaks early and then closes below its own starting low has "failed", which points to a lower low at the next degree up. That is when he turns short or waits for the bigger low.

Every thesis carries an invalidation stated in advance. The most important is a **higher-high close** above the prior swing high: for Bitcoin, the ~83K "most important level in finance". "Invalidations exist for a reason", and when one fires he flips. A hand-drawn "yellow squiggle" shows the path he expects. A stock-market **alarm gate** decides whether dips are bought: failed daily cycles first, then trendline breakdowns, then high-yield credit spreads blowing out or the yield curves sweeping up. News is treated as following the cycles: "show me the charts and I'll tell you the news".

## Context / higher timeframe / regime

- **Nesting fixes the degree, and the degree sets the bias of the one below.** If BTC is in its 4-year decline, bounces are counter-trend. If stocks are in a 4-year up-leg, daily cycle lows (DCLs) are buys. Bitcoin's phase governs ETH, XRP and the other alts, which "exacerbate Bitcoin's downside" and "are just following Bitcoin into their lows". Each asset has its own multi-year cycle. [Another FOMC Shakeout, 2026-07-28, 14:49](https://youtu.be/wyoC1nHivA8?t=889), [New Lows Coming For Crypto, 2026-08-11, 10:12](https://youtu.be/UcAX4VpRTfg?t=612), [Here's What's Next !!, 2026-10-09, 05:46](https://youtu.be/9FrE_d7KaFc?t=346)
- **Nesting also dates the big low.** He rejected an October 2026 BTC 4-year low because "having two dailies inside of a weekly just doesn't really fit with what we've seen for the rest of this cycle". In his framing, nesting "doesn't actually give us an expected four-year low date until much later". [It's starting, 2026-08-01, 17:57](https://youtu.be/L3QWk203NR4?t=1077), [New Lows Coming For Crypto, 2026-08-11, 02:36](https://youtu.be/UcAX4VpRTfg?t=156), [It's happening, 2026-09-12, 23:03](https://youtu.be/ZpYL6r2JzuY?t=1383)
- **Tolerance of the 4-year (and yearly) low: plus or minus one weekly cycle.** "Four-year cycle lows don't really move." He later uses this to call the 16 Aug 2026 BTC low a legal early 4-year low: it was inside tolerance, while the 2022 trap high was outside it. [Let's be Brutally Honest, 2026-08-15, 01:35](https://youtu.be/9-ISGikTF4Y?t=95), [Major Opportunity for the Long term account, 2026-09-17, 11:01](https://youtu.be/ZlYblgqzJ2w?t=661), [Bitcoin - Dip or Lockout Rally ??, 2026-09-23, 09:17](https://youtu.be/l8tdmwnJxSk?t=557)
- **Trend-follow the higher timeframe until it breaks.** Long stocks "until something goes bang": "I am a trend follower". Bullish "until cycles start to fail". One red daily candle never changes his stance, and two or three days "seldom" do. [It's starting, 2026-08-01, 01:03](https://youtu.be/L3QWk203NR4?t=63), [The Next Move Will Shock Investors, 2026-09-10, 02:37](https://youtu.be/cp7MHZZBFW8?t=157), [Investors are Quitting at the Worst Time, 2026-09-11, 00:30](https://youtu.be/V55qMPLctPE?t=30)
- **Structure definitions.** Below the prior swing high, price is making lower highs and lower lows: "That's what a downtrend is". A bull market needs "a significant higher low followed by a higher high". [Could it be, 2026-08-22, 13:43](https://youtu.be/8ZbQVOShDco?t=823), [FOMC Shakeout / Trap & GO !!, 2026-09-16, 04:42](https://youtu.be/28wXrOWuVyA?t=282), [We've Reached a Major Inflection Point, 2026-09-30, 06:49](https://youtu.be/O2PXnNlV3gA?t=409)
- **Translation (same definition in 2023 and 2026).** Measure low to low and mark the midpoint. A peak to the left is left-translated (bearish), a peak to the right is right-translated (bullish). A peak exactly in the middle is "neutral or leaning towards a bullish" cycle (2023). [HOW TO TRADE CYCLES, 2023-08-30, 07:55](https://youtu.be/XWTIrduvgrU?t=475), [Left Translation, MSTR & Vertical Accumulation, 2026-08-28, 02:01](https://youtu.be/CgNaNridcaY?t=121)
- **Shape of a weekly cycle.** 2023: the first few daily cycles right-translate, then the last left-translates and fails into the weekly cycle low; "weekly and yearly cycle lows… typically end with a failure of a daily and weekly cycle low". 2026, one degree up: "three rights and a left" make a larger cycle, and after three right-translated 4-year cycles "oftentimes the fourth will left translate". BTC's cycle 4 was right-translated, so he expected cycle 5 to left-translate. [HOW TO TRADE CYCLES, 2023-08-30, 06:21](https://youtu.be/XWTIrduvgrU?t=381), [HOW TO TRADE CYCLES, 2023-08-30, 13:05](https://youtu.be/XWTIrduvgrU?t=785), [Left Translation, MSTR & Vertical Accumulation, 2026-08-28, 03:05](https://youtu.be/CgNaNridcaY?t=185), [Left Translation, MSTR & Vertical Accumulation, 2026-08-28, 03:35](https://youtu.be/CgNaNridcaY?t=215)
- **Right translation implies a harder, faster decline.** An extremely right-translated stock cycle (the C-19 analogue, "top month 48, where we should be bottoming") has to "puke into the low" and correct "hard and fast", taking "12 weeks or less" instead of the normal year. (This is already queued as `right-translation-predicts-decline-shape`.) [It's Started, 2026-08-04, 08:53](https://youtu.be/JoRNW_4jCVc?t=533), [The Next Move Will Shock Investors, 2026-09-10, 04:13](https://youtu.be/cp7MHZZBFW8?t=253), [Was it all a Trap ??, 2026-09-24, 03:11](https://youtu.be/eZ7x38UEdOk?t=191)
- **Inversions mean seller exhaustion.** In 2023: an inversion forces the half-cycle low to become the daily cycle low, usually "after intense flushing of price at the end of a weekly cycle"; they are "very very rare". In 2026: "Inversions only happen when we exhaust the sellers", and an inverted weekly cycle is "even more bullish". [HOW TO TRADE CYCLES, 2023-08-30, 13:38](https://youtu.be/XWTIrduvgrU?t=818), [Could it be, 2026-08-22, 12:41](https://youtu.be/8ZbQVOShDco?t=761), [Here's Why, 2026-09-18, 11:25](https://youtu.be/NUaGWQQTN5Y?t=685)
- **Failure cascades upward.** Two failed daily cycles back to back imply a failed weekly cycle. A failed weekly cycle means the asset is "seeking a four-year cycle low at the next weekly cycle", and that low should come below the failed cycle's low. [The Next Leg Higher has Started!, 2026-10-03, 20:08](https://youtu.be/IYfTEZtA2f8?t=1208), [How I outperformed Bitcoin last cycle, 2026-10-14, 02:37](https://youtu.be/52ZlNcndcSE?t=157)
- **The 80% rule (first stated 2026-10-13).** If a confirmed DCL near a candidate 4-year low goes on to make new highs, there is "about 80% probability the four-year cycle low is in". If it left-translates and fails, "which means it closes below wherever this cycle low confirms", there is an 80% probability the low is not in. [It's Happening!, 2026-10-13, 06:18](https://youtu.be/qarjzOUTk8k?t=378), [How I outperformed Bitcoin last cycle, 2026-10-14, 10:00](https://youtu.be/52ZlNcndcSE?t=600), [This Changes Things, 2026-10-17, 04:45](https://youtu.be/l4d4MOUG1Gg?t=285)
- **A higher-high close starts a new regime**, one "where we're going to go up more than we come down", in distance and in time. Weekly pivots stay "approximately equidistant", so the next weekly window does not move; only whether that low comes in higher or lower changes. [The Next Leg Higher has Started!, 2026-10-03, 03:11](https://youtu.be/IYfTEZtA2f8?t=191), [The Next Leg Higher has Started!, 2026-10-03, 03:42](https://youtu.be/IYfTEZtA2f8?t=222), [AI Threat Arrives on Schedule, 2026-10-08, 06:29](https://youtu.be/xbRQulPGwkQ?t=389)
- **An early low is harder to trade than a late one.** Until price is "well past this window", a later 4-year low stays possible. Versions of the end date: "well past February of 2027" (8 Sep), "early next year" (14 Sep), "well into about Q2 of next year" (24 Sep), "Once we get past kind of like Q1 of next year, we'll be able to say with absolute certainty" (9 Oct). [Left Translation, MSTR & Vertical Accumulation, 2026-08-28, 07:44](https://youtu.be/CgNaNridcaY?t=464), [The Bitcoin Bart is Still Valid, 2026-09-08, 08:23](https://youtu.be/fDMJEhj2CGo?t=503), [Simulation Confirmed, Narrative Arrives, 2026-09-14, 05:19](https://youtu.be/LK5YLwkwCiI?t=319), [Was it all a Trap ??, 2026-09-24, 12:53](https://youtu.be/eZ7x38UEdOk?t=773), [Here's What's Next !!, 2026-10-09, 06:50](https://youtu.be/9FrE_d7KaFc?t=410)
- **Late in a 4-year cycle, widen tolerance**: "a degree of flexibility here that is abnormally high relative to normal cycles". [It's Make or Break, 2026-08-03, 08:03](https://youtu.be/jweSBXongzE?t=483)
- **Where BTC stood in the cycle in 2025.** Measured from the bear-market low, the 2017 and 2021 cycles and the current one all topped "around that kind of month 35 topping window". On 27 Aug 2025 he said ~95% of the bull phase was used up. He called betting that a 4-year low will simply not come "absolutely ludicrous". [The 4 Year Cycle is 95% Complete..., 2025-08-27, 06:36](https://youtu.be/9-JtVbT-4-o?t=396), [The 4 Year Cycle is 95% Complete..., 2025-08-27, 08:39](https://youtu.be/9-JtVbT-4-o?t=519), [The 4 Year Cycle is 95% Complete..., 2025-08-27, 09:11](https://youtu.be/9-JtVbT-4-o?t=551)
- **Altcoins are higher beta to BTC (8 Sep 2026).** Alts can make expanded-flat new highs while BTC prints lower highs; "whatever Bitcoin does, the alts will do". [The Bitcoin Bart is Still Valid, 2026-09-08, 02:33](https://youtu.be/fDMJEhj2CGo?t=153), [The Bitcoin Bart is Still Valid, 2026-09-08, 04:07](https://youtu.be/fDMJEhj2CGo?t=247)
- **Market stages (2022; quoted again 2026-09-25).**
  - 2022: stage 1 is accumulation, stage 2 the markup, stage 3 distribution, stage 4 the markdown; "we do not want to sit in a stock during stages one three or four".
  - 2026-09-25 (quoting Cheds, using the 30-week MA): "I used to use a lot of stage analysis and it is extremely rare in my experience that you don't get any oscillation in stage one."
  - [How to Find and Trade Momentum Stocks, 2022-08-23, 00:30](https://youtu.be/Tl1ObCPO9m0?t=30), [How to Find and Trade Momentum Stocks, 2022-08-23, 01:02](https://youtu.be/Tl1ObCPO9m0?t=62), [The Blow Off Top Continues, 2026-09-25, 02:35](https://youtu.be/luWzbwZ02mg?t=155), [The Blow Off Top Continues, 2026-09-25, 04:14](https://youtu.be/luWzbwZ02mg?t=254)
- **Hyperwave (2025).** He said US stocks were in hyperwave phase 4, and "when hyper waves violate phase 4 they tend to return to phase one", "about 70% on the way down". [The 4 Year Cycle is 95% Complete..., 2025-08-27, 13:47](https://youtu.be/9-JtVbT-4-o?t=827)
- **Hypothesis discipline (2024 principles).**
  - "admit that you don't know anything"; take it "one day at a time"; abandon "the need to be right"; "put in the work".
  - "Define your hypothesis figure out where invalidation occurs and then sit there with it", and test it daily.
  - [Principles for staying ahead in investing, 2024-01-30, 06:37](https://youtu.be/4wc4EOj6KOo?t=397), [Principles for staying ahead in investing, 2024-01-30, 09:39](https://youtu.be/4wc4EOj6KOo?t=579), [Principles for staying ahead in investing, 2024-01-30, 13:45](https://youtu.be/4wc4EOj6KOo?t=825), [Principles for staying ahead in investing, 2024-01-30, 14:15](https://youtu.be/4wc4EOj6KOo?t=855)
- **Order of tops between markets (2 Oct onward):** yields top first, then stocks about "three to four months" later, then commodities (2000, 2007). A yield top "sets off the countdown timer for the final bull leg". [A Bullish Q4 is Loading !!, 2026-10-02, 02:41](https://youtu.be/MG8WI9O0Eyk?t=161), [Here's my Trading Plan for the Week, 2026-10-05, 00:58](https://youtu.be/fMQyp-wqH8Y?t=58), [Here's my Trading Plan for the Week, 2026-10-05, 01:35](https://youtu.be/fMQyp-wqH8Y?t=95)

In late September the projected lows of yields, gold, oil, stocks, DXY and maybe BTC clustered in December 2026 to February 2027. - **Narrative follows cycles, and its size scales with the cycle degree.** A presidential tweet comes with a daily low, a surprise data print or hike with a weekly low, a "sky is falling" story with a multi-year low. Earnings, the Clarity Act and other news are not vetoes: "price is dictated by the cycles, not the balance sheet". - **Breadth broadening into a top is unusual and, here, bullish.** The median US stock was at a 5-year high and more than 70% of NDX stocks were above their 200-day MA, which he read as fuel for the blow-off. - **His weekly self-audit is periodic.** Every Saturday "deep dive" reruns "the base case hypothesis" (25 Jul, 1 Aug, 8 Aug, 15 Aug, 22 Aug, 12 Sep, 26 Sep, 3 Oct, 17 Oct; 5 Sep was a Saturday Elliott-wave special). [It's starting, 2026-08-01, 00:30](https://youtu.be/L3QWk203NR4?t=30), [Let's be Brutally Honest, 2026-08-15, 04:13](https://youtu.be/9-ISGikTF4Y?t=253)

## Location (the timing window first, levels second)

- **The location is a time window, not a price.** "No one really cares about the price here. We're looking for the high probability low to manage the risk around." "We're not searching for a number to buy. We are searching for an area on the chart to manage risk around." "whether it's a higher low or a lower low or even a double bottom doesn't make a difference to me. I'm just looking to get inside of that window." The arrow he draws "pertains to time, not price". [It's Started, 2026-08-04, 04:11](https://youtu.be/JoRNW_4jCVc?t=251), [The Treasury Just Blinked, 2026-08-20, 11:46](https://youtu.be/_Gn7BpBvA44?t=706), [Here's my Trading Plan for the Week, 2026-10-05, 02:47](https://youtu.be/fMQyp-wqH8Y?t=167), [FOMC Shakeout / Trap & GO !!, 2026-09-16, 02:41](https://youtu.be/28wXrOWuVyA?t=161)
- **How the window is built.** Average every low-to-low span over the asset's whole history and apply a 10% tolerance, giving 54–66 days for BTC's 60-day cycle (see Indicator settings). The low should ideally come in the middle of the window. Once it is set inside the year-end window, it "should not be violated again for the remainder of the bull run". [Answering Your Questions, 2026-08-18, 08:20](https://youtu.be/EKSjKkcIIqU?t=500), [HOW TO TRADE CYCLES, 2023-08-30, 02:33](https://youtu.be/XWTIrduvgrU?t=153), [Was it all a Trap ??, 2026-09-24, 13:23](https://youtu.be/eZ7x38UEdOk?t=803)
- **Never buy on the window alone.** "Sometimes cycles will extend significantly beyond the timing window", especially "in very bearish conditions" (2023). A late, time-based consolidation inside the window is "higher probability" than an early low. [HOW TO TRADE CYCLES, 2023-08-30, 11:03](https://youtu.be/XWTIrduvgrU?t=663), [It's Started, 2026-08-04, 04:11](https://youtu.be/JoRNW_4jCVc?t=251)
- **The "first hurdle"** is the swing high after a cycle low. Until price clears it there is no higher high and no credible bottom. [It's a trap, 2026-07-27, 08:45](https://youtu.be/C3E_zU5a6c4?t=525), [The Lows Finally Confirmed !!, 2026-07-31, 04:40](https://youtu.be/bkf_ALW8W_A?t=280), [Gold at Critical Inflection Point !!, 2026-08-10, 08:02](https://youtu.be/MMnkrbLKZRc?t=482)
- **Preferred long location: the breakout-retest higher low.** After a break of a descending trendline or a range top, he buys the first higher low ("breakout, retest, resumption") with the stop under it. [More Upside Ahead, 2026-08-13, 03:14](https://youtu.be/ggtv4-O6Gdw?t=194), [The Treasury Just Blinked, 2026-08-20, 07:33](https://youtu.be/_Gn7BpBvA44?t=453), [Bitcoin - Dip or Lockout Rally ??, 2026-09-23, 17:25](https://youtu.be/l8tdmwnJxSk?t=1045)
- **BTC's "most important level in finance"** is the prior swing-high cluster at 82–83K ("about 82 and change, 82.8 or something like that", 5 Sep). In October he calls it only "the red", with no number spoken. [A NEW BULL MARKET HAS FORMED, 2026-08-21, 07:28](https://youtu.be/BHMj3gGjT-Q?t=448), [The Most Important Level In Finance, 2026-08-26, 03:05](https://youtu.be/c46MwOOnips?t=185), [Bitcoin has 3 Possible Paths from here, 2026-09-05, 03:11](https://youtu.be/MK47H-8RTBo?t=191), [AI Threat Arrives on Schedule, 2026-10-08, 07:32](https://youtu.be/xbRQulPGwkQ?t=452)
- **BTC price zones after he flipped bullish.**
  - 16 Sep gate for the expected 2–3 Oct DCL: a low "above about 70K" means a breakout is likely; "below about 65K" means "left trans and failure".
  - 25 Sep: "anywhere down into this kind of 65 to 70 neighborhood that will still be close enough to the bottom to call it the bottom".
  - 2 Oct: "low 70s, high 60s handle" = "smash buying".
  - 14 Oct: wave-2 completion "anywhere on a seven handle… six handle… 60 or 70K".
  - [FOMC Shakeout / Trap & GO !!, 2026-09-16, 04:12](https://youtu.be/28wXrOWuVyA?t=252), [FOMC Shakeout / Trap & GO !!, 2026-09-16, 05:46](https://youtu.be/28wXrOWuVyA?t=346), [The Blow Off Top Continues, 2026-09-25, 01:33](https://youtu.be/luWzbwZ02mg?t=93), [A Bullish Q4 is Loading !!, 2026-10-02, 07:30](https://youtu.be/MG8WI9O0Eyk?t=450), [How I outperformed Bitcoin last cycle, 2026-10-14, 09:29](https://youtu.be/52ZlNcndcSE?t=569)
- **Hand-drawn decision lines.** Examples: gold's descending red resistance line and "bull market breakout level", the 10y "blue range", the TLT "box", the stocks' "red legacy trendline". Gold also has to clear its 200-day MA. These are retrospective (hand-drawn). [Time to LOCK IN !!, 2026-08-05, 09:15](https://youtu.be/jiaoYL45IrM?t=555), [More Upside Ahead, 2026-08-13, 04:14](https://youtu.be/ggtv4-O6Gdw?t=254), [Did Gold Just Show What's Next for Bitcoin?, 2026-09-28, 04:55](https://youtu.be/JBHwqCwJma8?t=295), [What is Going on ??, 2026-09-15, 01:20](https://youtu.be/wqa0EcxKOYY?t=80)
- **TLT bottom zone, by date.** ~80, with a sweep wanted (Aug) → "78 to 80" (from 24 Sep) → the low came at 77.87 (stated 9 Oct). On 30 Sep he says he had always said 78–80. [Lower CPI to Ignite a Rally, 2026-08-12, 02:39](https://youtu.be/yS7VStJ7S8k?t=159), [Was it all a Trap ??, 2026-09-24, 19:29](https://youtu.be/eZ7x38UEdOk?t=1169), [We've Reached a Major Inflection Point, 2026-09-30, 04:40](https://youtu.be/O2PXnNlV3gA?t=280), [Here's What's Next !!, 2026-10-09, 06:18](https://youtu.be/9FrE_d7KaFc?t=378)
- **Wyckoff sweeps and springs.** A sweep and deviation of a range low is where he adds (TLT), and if the range resolves up "the low is the spring". A schematic is valid only with "a matching fingerprint volume profile" (credited to Hot Potato Trader). [FOMC was a Nothing Burger - Now What ??, 2026-07-30, 11:08](https://youtu.be/es-NQ1p-dC4?t=668), [The Most Important Level In Finance, 2026-08-26, 01:32](https://youtu.be/c46MwOOnips?t=92), [A Massive Week Ahead, 2026-08-31, 03:48](https://youtu.be/-HDKylerUWo?t=228)
- **Fibonacci only incidentally** ("I'm not really a fib guy"): semis at a 50% retracement, miners' "golden pocket" at 88/83. [FOMC Arrives! Here's How I am Trading it, 2026-07-29, 02:33](https://youtu.be/nKpG-HTBRrQ?t=153), [The Funniest Analysis I've Ever Seen!, 2026-09-02, 13:53](https://youtu.be/DPhLPRVnliM?t=833)
- **Gaps below a breakout** are targets that may be back-filled into the next DCL. [New Lows Coming For Crypto, 2026-08-11, 08:39](https://youtu.be/UcAX4VpRTfg?t=519), [The Treasury Just Blinked, 2026-08-20, 11:46](https://youtu.be/_Gn7BpBvA44?t=706)
- **Momentum stocks (2022): the location is a weekly "box".** A box is the high and low of a base. It counts only after the trend template passes (see Entry triggers). "The more bases there are the more likely they are to fail." [How to Find and Trade Momentum Stocks, 2022-08-23, 08:38](https://youtu.be/Tl1ObCPO9m0?t=518), [How to Find and Trade Momentum Stocks, 2022-08-23, 12:42](https://youtu.be/Tl1ObCPO9m0?t=762)

## Entry triggers

- **Long = a DCL confirmed inside the window.** "The indicator will pick that low and upon confirmation, we should be long." Two examples: US100 on 31 Jul ("We're above the 10 SMA. We've got a confirmation… We're in"), and the Dow on 29 Jul ("medium confluence"). [It's a trap, 2026-07-27, 13:29](https://youtu.be/C3E_zU5a6c4?t=809), [The Lows Finally Confirmed !!, 2026-07-31, 08:22](https://youtu.be/bkf_ALW8W_A?t=502), [FOMC Arrives! Here's How I am Trading it, 2026-07-29, 02:03](https://youtu.be/nKpG-HTBRrQ?t=123)
- **Never on the window or a guess alone.** "I don't just assume and buy… Always wait for the confirmation signals to fire." FOMC weeks bring shakeouts, so he does not jump in early. He expects the first big green candle off the low to make the indicator label the swing. [FOMC Shakeout / Trap & GO !!, 2026-09-16, 08:22](https://youtu.be/28wXrOWuVyA?t=502), [FOMC Shakeout / Trap & GO !!, 2026-09-16, 08:54](https://youtu.be/28wXrOWuVyA?t=534), [FOMC Shakeout / Trap & GO !!, 2026-09-16, 07:21](https://youtu.be/28wXrOWuVyA?t=441). The same rule in 2025: "we don't just stab in the dark and say, 'Well, it's day 60. Time to buy.'" [The 4 Year Cycle is 95% Complete..., 2025-08-27, 05:04](https://youtu.be/9-JtVbT-4-o?t=304)
- **Later rather than earlier: a standing rule.** "I'm willing to give up 500 points in here in exchange for increased confidence" (gold). "I am going to be later to this trade in exchange for increased confidence." Buying the higher low is "better than having to buy the confirmation down here with a stop below the low": the stop is tighter, so "we can wind on more leverage whilst keeping the risk managed". [Time to LOCK IN !!, 2026-08-05, 06:41](https://youtu.be/jiaoYL45IrM?t=401), [More Upside Ahead, 2026-08-13, 10:07](https://youtu.be/ggtv4-O6Gdw?t=607), [Record Shorts, 2026-08-19, 05:38](https://youtu.be/O2e2PpZn6vA?t=338)
- **After a breakout, a higher low is the entry; a slam back into the range is not.** For silver, "that's going to be my signal for entry and managing risk around that higher low". [New Lows Coming For Crypto, 2026-08-11, 08:09](https://youtu.be/UcAX4VpRTfg?t=489), [New Lows Coming For Crypto, 2026-08-11, 09:10](https://youtu.be/UcAX4VpRTfg?t=550)
- **How gold approaches its DCL**: the 10 SMA flattens or turns down, then "some sort of two drives pattern into the window. And then it should be time to go". [Record Shorts, 2026-08-19, 06:10](https://youtu.be/O2e2PpZn6vA?t=370)
- **Monthly 4-year-low entry**: "buying this candle close above the 10 SMA on the monthly time frame with a couple of weekly closes above all of the aforementioned levels". [Does Sentiment Match a Bottom, 2026-08-25, 14:07](https://youtu.be/_freGHQfGEc?t=847)
- **Equity half-cycle low.** A big green close around day 20 ("especially with a big green candle today, then I would say the half cycle loads in and confirmed"). In October: "Today is a perfect day 20… all we need now is the trendline breakout", confirmed "so long as we close the week with a swing in place". [The Most Important Level In Finance, 2026-08-26, 07:12](https://youtu.be/c46MwOOnips?t=432), [How I outperformed Bitcoin last cycle, 2026-10-14, 13:07](https://youtu.be/52ZlNcndcSE?t=787), [And No One Believes It..., 2026-10-15, 04:43](https://youtu.be/hhb_9awG53c?t=283)
- **Weekly pivot (Dow, 17 Oct).** A weekly close "about 600 points higher" is "a confirmed weekly pivot", and then it is time to add equity exposure. [This Changes Things, 2026-10-17, 18:02](https://youtu.be/l4d4MOUG1Gg?t=1082)
- **TLT's three-step turn (15 Oct).** Break the red resistance line, get back inside the blue rectangle and hold it, then break the next red line. He also needs "next month's candle to be big and green". [And No One Believes It..., 2026-10-15, 14:09](https://youtu.be/hhb_9awG53c?t=849), [Here's What's Next !!, 2026-10-09, 08:26](https://youtu.be/9FrE_d7KaFc?t=506)
- **After an invalidation flips him bullish, buy the first pullback.** "It's a I'm buying every dip going forward environment"; after a break, "every dip is for buying… Whether it's a daily cycle low or weekly cycle low". After a higher-high close he waits for the higher low and buys it. [We're So Back !!, 2026-09-22, 02:02](https://youtu.be/n3R4fE6denY?t=122), [We're So Back, 2026-09-09, 13:38](https://youtu.be/O_zc6fm1I4g?t=818), [Could it be, 2026-08-22, 13:43](https://youtu.be/8ZbQVOShDco?t=823)
- **Short = left translation, then failure.** If the daily cycle peaks early and breaks below its starting low, "we'll hit the short". His BTC short "from just below 80k" (79,108) came from "a day 10 left translation followed by failure, followed by a lower high". Stocks are shorted only on "consolidations and sideways chop and failed cycles" or a lower high and rollover. In a Bart Simpson range: "get some shorts on into this low and then cover them and reallocate". [Another FOMC Shakeout, 2026-07-28, 07:43](https://youtu.be/wyoC1nHivA8?t=463), [Another FOMC Shakeout, 2026-07-28, 14:49](https://youtu.be/wyoC1nHivA8?t=889), [It's Make or Break, 2026-08-03, 05:29](https://youtu.be/jweSBXongzE?t=329), [New Lows Coming For Crypto, 2026-08-11, 07:08](https://youtu.be/UcAX4VpRTfg?t=428), [Could it be, 2026-08-22, 04:13](https://youtu.be/8ZbQVOShDco?t=253)
- **Contrarian 4-year-low entries.** ETH after a sweep of its prior 4-year low (~$900, "10R"). MSTR "on a trendline break" after a spill. BTC-linked equities at their own 4-year low after their weekly cycle has failed. [FOMC Arrives! Here's How I am Trading it, 2026-07-29, 06:41](https://youtu.be/nKpG-HTBRrQ?t=401), [I'm Sure About This, 2026-07-25, 18:31](https://youtu.be/Mm5NUZElP-Y?t=1111), [How I outperformed Bitcoin last cycle, 2026-10-14, 05:15](https://youtu.be/52ZlNcndcSE?t=315)
- **"Take a shot to land a shot" at a binary point.** Take a small long at the confirmed DCL with a stop under the lows, so that "worst case scenario, you pay the 2% stop". "I would rather pay a 2% stop loss here, see left translation and failure, head down here and buy heavy, than just assume that's what's coming, not get long, and then potentially get locked out." [Major Opportunity for the Long term account, 2026-09-17, 20:30](https://youtu.be/ZlYblgqzJ2w?t=1230), [Here's What's Next !!, 2026-10-09, 13:00](https://youtu.be/9FrE_d7KaFc?t=780), [And No One Believes It..., 2026-10-15, 08:57](https://youtu.be/hhb_9awG53c?t=537)
- **The two accounts enter differently.**
  - The long-term account may buy the first confirmed low.
  - For the short-term account, "I prefer to kind of have an established uptrend already… and a confirmed bottom".
  - Long-term "averaging in" is allowed only *inside* a yearly or 4-year window. TLT example: 10% about one weekly cycle before the low was due, then another 10% at the next weekly cycle.
  - [Answering Your Questions, 2026-08-18, 05:18](https://youtu.be/EKSjKkcIIqU?t=318), [Major Opportunity for the Long term account, 2026-09-17, 11:31](https://youtu.be/ZlYblgqzJ2w?t=691), [Major Opportunity for the Long term account, 2026-09-17, 12:02](https://youtu.be/ZlYblgqzJ2w?t=722)
- **Do not chase one big candle**: "There is no miss the move just because you saw one big green candle." [Time to LOCK IN !!, 2026-08-05, 08:12](https://youtu.be/jiaoYL45IrM?t=492)
- **The 2022 momentum entry (stocks; his adaptation of Mark Minervini's trend template).**
  - The trend template must pass first: price above the 50-, 150- and 200-day MAs; the 150 above the 200; the 200 "trending up for at least a month"; the 50 above the 150 and the 200; price above the 50.
  - Then buy the breakout above the weekly box. This is "buying new highs", "the opposite to buying the dip".
  - Candidates come from a watch list of stage-1 bases with alerts set above the horizontals, or from screeners for 52-week highs.
  - [How to Find and Trade Momentum Stocks, 2022-08-23, 03:02](https://youtu.be/Tl1ObCPO9m0?t=182), [How to Find and Trade Momentum Stocks, 2022-08-23, 07:37](https://youtu.be/Tl1ObCPO9m0?t=457), [How to Find and Trade Momentum Stocks, 2022-08-23, 02:02](https://youtu.be/Tl1ObCPO9m0?t=122), [How to Find and Trade Momentum Stocks, 2022-08-23, 05:03](https://youtu.be/Tl1ObCPO9m0?t=303)
- **Buy signal in the 2024 leverage example (QQQ)**: a breakout above the trendline "on a daily closing basis", with the stop below the local lows. [How to Correctly Apply Leverage in Trading, 2024-03-14, 03:36](https://youtu.be/Jxr8jrpexec?t=216), [How to Correctly Apply Leverage in Trading, 2024-03-14, 04:07](https://youtu.be/Jxr8jrpexec?t=247)
- **Late for confidence (2024, to Andrew)**: "I'm happy to be quite late to a trade in exchange for confidence". He was flat on BTC swing positions but kept the core allocation. [Cycle Trading with Andrew (@GoldnGuitars), 2024-08-10, 35:55](https://youtu.be/KSum66r_w8s?t=2155), [Cycle Trading with Andrew (@GoldnGuitars), 2024-08-10, 36:25](https://youtu.be/KSum66r_w8s?t=2185)

## Confirmations (things that raise conviction)

**The confirmation checklist, by date. It was never fixed: the count and which items are required changed over time.**

The six July items cannot be identified exactly from the audio. Part 1's best reading is: swing low, close above the 10 SMA, close above the down-trendline, Bressert cross, indicator low, and a big thrust or engulfing candle. Parabolic SAR first appears on 18 Aug (as a trailing-stop option) and on 31 Aug (it "helps"), so it is probably not one of the core six. The higher-high close is a separate master gate for calling a *bull market* (see Vetoes). It is not a checklist item for a DCL trade.

Other things that raise conviction:
- **Oscillator divergence at the big lows.**
  - A weekly oscillator that gives a bullish cross, "quickly uncrosses", then makes a higher low against a lower price low means the metals low is "on".
  - A monthly bullish divergence on TLT is "synonymous with bottoms" and "the hallmark of a major monthly or multi-year pivot".
  - TLT has "about three years of bullish divergence".
  - [The Lows Finally Confirmed !!, 2026-07-31, 06:19](https://youtu.be/bkf_ALW8W_A?t=379), [The Lows Finally Confirmed !!, 2026-07-31, 06:49](https://youtu.be/bkf_ALW8W_A?t=409), [It's Happening Again !! (Asset Cycles Aligning), 2026-09-07, 12:49](https://youtu.be/OqzFvsbmfGE?t=769), [Evidence of a New Crypto Bull is Mounting, 2026-08-24, 12:01](https://youtu.be/5m-qim84ijY?t=721)
- **His indicator's inversion or degree call.** "The indicator thinks we inverted the weekly cycle here. Normally when it does this, it usually is right." [Record Shorts, 2026-08-19, 06:10](https://youtu.be/O2e2PpZn6vA?t=370)
- **Leaders lead.**
  - The Dow led the July DCL. The Russell leads bull-flag breaks.
  - Semis are the canary: "without the semis moving to new highs here, we will likely struggle to get the rest of the indices to move higher". Mag 7 and semis base breakouts give "enough fuel".
  - Miners lead metals.
  - MSTR should lead BTC bottoms. BTC-equity "subtle tells" (MSTR lower high, RIOT in range, MARA lower highs) argued in late September that BTC was not done.
  - BKLN leads the S&P.
  - [FOMC Arrives! Here's How I am Trading it, 2026-07-29, 01:33](https://youtu.be/nKpG-HTBRrQ?t=93), [More Upside Ahead, 2026-08-13, 06:49](https://youtu.be/ggtv4-O6Gdw?t=409), [The Wait is Almost Over, 2026-08-17, 04:48](https://youtu.be/Q_U-MbBmLdI?t=288), [We're So Back !!, 2026-09-22, 09:50](https://youtu.be/n3R4fE6denY?t=590), [Bitcoin - Dip or Lockout Rally ??, 2026-09-23, 17:25](https://youtu.be/l8tdmwnJxSk?t=1045), [Does Sentiment Match a Bottom, 2026-08-25, 17:09](https://youtu.be/_freGHQfGEc?t=1029), [Huge Announcement Coming Today, 2026-09-29, 07:52](https://youtu.be/0zlHZQzW28Q?t=472), [We are printing again, 2026-08-07, 01:04](https://youtu.be/ORSNkP6I4jI?t=64)
- **ETH "leading or lying".** An ETH higher high means nothing without a BTC higher high. On 12 and 14 Sep the test was concrete: ETH must fill the upper wick of its breakout candle "in the coming week", or it is lying. On 27 Aug: if ETH reaches ~2,800, BTC is "soon to join"; if BTC fails, ETH falls "significantly harder and faster". [We're So Back, 2026-09-09, 17:47](https://youtu.be/O_zc6fm1I4g?t=1067), [It's happening, 2026-09-12, 04:22](https://youtu.be/ZpYL6r2JzuY?t=262), [Simulation Confirmed, Narrative Arrives, 2026-09-14, 04:16](https://youtu.be/LK5YLwkwCiI?t=256), [Crypto Bull Market Confirms Here, 2026-08-27, 08:01](https://youtu.be/RevmcdyF-5s?t=481)
- **Sentiment must "match" the low.** At a 4-year low he expects fear and capitulation; mocking and victory laps are "not how the lows occur". He says "We don't have to have sentiment extremes at inflection points, but typically for four year cycle lows, we do". Sentiment is "the number one way" to tell a Bart Simpson top (toxic triumph on each push) from a consolidation that will break up (quiet doubt). [A NEW BULL MARKET HAS FORMED, 2026-08-21, 05:50](https://youtu.be/BHMj3gGjT-Q?t=350), [The Most Important Level In Finance, 2026-08-26, 04:38](https://youtu.be/c46MwOOnips?t=278), [WE Are Gonna Make It !!, 2026-09-04, 01:33](https://youtu.be/f8JJ3myLgR8?t=93), [FOMC Shakeout / Trap & GO !!, 2026-09-16, 05:12](https://youtu.be/28wXrOWuVyA?t=312)
- **Named sentiment and positioning reads.** None of these is his own data.
  - AAII: bears ~42% with breadth strong (Jul); 53% bearish on 18 Sep ("55 to 60 would be considered extremely bearish"; the GFC low "only had 70%").
  - Options skew as at the 2025 low.
  - Record asset-manager Nasdaq futures shorts read as squeeze fuel.
  - Money-market / cash ETF flows (3-month average) "synonymous with the lows".
  - TLT retail inflow of $61M in one day.
  - Share of stocks with RSI < 30 at its highest since March 2025.
  - Henrik's 10y bearish-sentiment reading at 10 ("the last two times… major cyclical tops").
  - [Another FOMC Shakeout, 2026-07-28, 02:35](https://youtu.be/wyoC1nHivA8?t=155), [Here's Why, 2026-09-18, 01:02](https://youtu.be/NUaGWQQTN5Y?t=62), [FOMC was a Nothing Burger - Now What ??, 2026-07-30, 05:51](https://youtu.be/es-NQ1p-dC4?t=351), [Record Shorts, 2026-08-19, 02:30](https://youtu.be/O2e2PpZn6vA?t=150), [Huge Announcement Coming Today, 2026-09-29, 06:18](https://youtu.be/0zlHZQzW28Q?t=378), [It's Looking Good!, 2026-10-06, 05:22](https://youtu.be/un3YVNZSEVQ?t=322), [The Most Crowded Trade, 2026-10-01, 09:10](https://youtu.be/fkncsQKAIp4?t=550), [A Bullish Q4 is Loading !!, 2026-10-02, 01:34](https://youtu.be/MG8WI9O0Eyk?t=94)
- **Breadth (quoted)**: "S&P 500 above the June peak combined with S&P 500 AD line above the uptrend line, you have to lean bullish". [Evidence of a New Crypto Bull is Mounting, 2026-08-24, 11:29](https://youtu.be/5m-qim84ijY?t=689)
- **BTC moving averages (adopted from Ben Cowen).**
  - BTC tends to dump into a golden cross. What matters is the next swing: a higher high together with a weekly close above the 50-week SMA is "a good predictor of bear market being over", while rejection there with a lower high means more lows.
  - On 21 Sep BTC was above the 50-week MA "for the first time in 45 weeks", and the 100-week MA (~89K) was "the next target".
  - [We're So Back, 2026-09-09, 06:41](https://youtu.be/O_zc6fm1I4g?t=401), [We're So Back, 2026-09-09, 07:14](https://youtu.be/O_zc6fm1I4g?t=434), [Feeling FOMO? Watch This!, 2026-09-21, 09:17](https://youtu.be/853X_T8T3Ow?t=557)
- **TLT RSI** "at the most oversold levels… in about the last eight or so years", after which TLT has rallied ~20% on average (60% in 2018). [Here's What's Next !!, 2026-10-09, 03:10](https://youtu.be/9FrE_d7KaFc?t=190)
- **Other people's cycle counts used as confluence** (Bob Loukas, Erlovsky, Tony, Andrew, Kimo). They are attributed and not used as his rules. [FOMC Shakeout / Trap & GO !!, 2026-09-16, 12:00](https://youtu.be/28wXrOWuVyA?t=720), [FOMC Shakeout / Trap & GO !!, 2026-09-16, 10:56](https://youtu.be/28wXrOWuVyA?t=656), [Huge Announcement Coming Today, 2026-09-29, 05:12](https://youtu.be/0zlHZQzW28Q?t=312)
- **A narrative arriving on schedule** is read as confirmation that the cycle, not the news, drives price. [Simulation Confirmed, Narrative Arrives, 2026-09-14, 01:09](https://youtu.be/LK5YLwkwCiI?t=69), [AI Threat Arrives on Schedule, 2026-10-08, 12:16](https://youtu.be/xbRQulPGwkQ?t=736)
- **Hot data with falling yields**: "CPI comes in hot and yields went down, not up." [And No One Believes It..., 2026-10-15, 04:09](https://youtu.be/hhb_9awG53c?t=249)
- **A forced liquidation in the window**: cycles act "as heat-seeking missiles for over-leveraged players". This is anecdotal. [The Lows Finally Confirmed !!, 2026-07-31, 02:33](https://youtu.be/bkf_ALW8W_A?t=153)

## Vetoes (things that cancel a trade whatever else says)

- **No bull call without a higher-high close.** "It is malpractice to be bearish above this line, but it is malpractice to be bullish whilst we are below it." He restated it in 9 of the 14 videos up to 10 Aug and kept it until it fired on 22 Sep. Wicks and "a quick deviation" do not count. (Versions by date are in the next section.) [It's a trap, 2026-07-27, 08:45](https://youtu.be/C3E_zU5a6c4?t=525), [Does Sentiment Match a Bottom, 2026-08-25, 13:07](https://youtu.be/_freGHQfGEc?t=787), [The Most Important Level In Finance, 2026-08-26, 03:05](https://youtu.be/c46MwOOnips?t=185), [Left Translation, MSTR & Vertical Accumulation, 2026-08-28, 01:31](https://youtu.be/CgNaNridcaY?t=91)
- **Clearing the first hurdle is not enough on its own**: "even if it does clear this first hurdle, it's likely to make a lower high… and then roll over into a four-year low" (10 Aug). [Gold at Critical Inflection Point !!, 2026-08-10, 11:13](https://youtu.be/MMnkrbLKZRc?t=673)
- **No inversion label until a new high.** [Time to LOCK IN !!, 2026-08-05, 09:15](https://youtu.be/jiaoYL45IrM?t=555)
- **No stock short without a printed-then-failed cycle**, "however bearish it feels". [Another FOMC Shakeout, 2026-07-28, 07:12](https://youtu.be/wyoC1nHivA8?t=432), [It's starting, 2026-08-01, 12:51](https://youtu.be/L3QWk203NR4?t=771)
- **No trade on an unconfirmed low**, even when price is "through the window", and especially around FOMC shakeouts. [Major Opportunity for the Long term account, 2026-09-17, 18:24](https://youtu.be/ZlYblgqzJ2w?t=1104), [FOMC Shakeout / Trap & GO !!, 2026-09-16, 08:54](https://youtu.be/28wXrOWuVyA?t=534)
- **No averaging down in the short-term account.** "Losers average losers" (quoting Minervini). Once stopped or once the cycle fails, do not add on the way down. [Major Opportunity for the Long term account, 2026-09-17, 05:18](https://youtu.be/ZlYblgqzJ2w?t=318)
- **No change of risk with conviction or after a losing streak** (see Sizing). [Major Opportunity for the Long term account, 2026-09-17, 03:11](https://youtu.be/ZlYblgqzJ2w?t=191)
- **RSI divergence on its own is not a signal**: "I don't care about RSI. I don't care about divergence"; it is not confirmed "until you get a swing and a follow through and a change in market structure". [It's a trap, 2026-07-27, 03:03](https://youtu.be/C3E_zU5a6c4?t=183)
- **Fundamentals, news and valuation are not vetoes** while the gate is green. He cites ~20× forward P/E on semis (Timmer) as showing no bubble. [FOMC was a Nothing Burger - Now What ??, 2026-07-30, 10:07](https://youtu.be/es-NQ1p-dC4?t=607), [FOMC Arrives! Here's How I am Trading it, 2026-07-29, 05:40](https://youtu.be/nKpG-HTBRrQ?t=340), [Was it all a Trap ??, 2026-09-24, 16:59](https://youtu.be/eZ7x38UEdOk?t=1019)
- **Sentiment that does not match a major low.** Returning crypto bullishness "is not normal for a major bottom". He calls it unusual for many beginners to be long a major gold low. [Time to LOCK IN !!, 2026-08-05, 09:45](https://youtu.be/jiaoYL45IrM?t=585), [Gold at Critical Inflection Point !!, 2026-08-10, 02:38](https://youtu.be/MMnkrbLKZRc?t=158)
- **Futures-led rallies fade (quoted)**: "a sustainable rally needs both spot and futures demand". [Lower CPI to Ignite a Rally, 2026-08-12, 08:15](https://youtu.be/yS7VStJ7S8k?t=495)
- **A Wyckoff schematic without its volume fingerprint is invalid.** [A Massive Week Ahead, 2026-08-31, 03:48](https://youtu.be/-HDKylerUWo?t=228), [A Massive Week Ahead, 2026-08-31, 04:19](https://youtu.be/-HDKylerUWo?t=259)
- **A semis lower high plus a failed cycle is a "real alarm signal" for all indices.** [The Wait is Almost Over, 2026-08-17, 04:48](https://youtu.be/Q_U-MbBmLdI?t=288), [The Wait is Almost Over, 2026-08-17, 05:20](https://youtu.be/Q_U-MbBmLdI?t=320)
- **FedWatch above 85% for a hike** means he treats the hike as certain and expects metals and crypto to fall. [And I Can Prove It !!, 2026-09-03, 08:04](https://youtu.be/aK_JCKqbMJQ?t=484), [Simulation Confirmed, Narrative Arrives, 2026-09-14, 00:33](https://youtu.be/LK5YLwkwCiI?t=33)
- **No metals buying until the weekly cycle low** (October): "still nothing to do in the precious metals until we get to the weekly cycle low". [It's Looking Good!, 2026-10-06, 07:28](https://youtu.be/un3YVNZSEVQ?t=448), [And No One Believes It..., 2026-10-15, 06:52](https://youtu.be/hhb_9awG53c?t=412)
- **Conditions on buying the dip (17 Oct)**: "so long as no funds get completely blown out and we don't see the [VIX] close the week somewhere up here". No VIX number is given. [This Changes Things, 2026-10-17, 18:35](https://youtu.be/l4d4MOUG1Gg?t=1115)
- **A new inflation regime needs a higher high *then* a higher low** on the inflation chart. [The Year End Everything Rally, 2026-09-26, 06:54](https://youtu.be/_gS-hSL_vOI?t=414)
- **No TA on broker charts**: TradingView only, and the broker is "just… an execution tool". [Rejected at Resistance, 2026-08-14, 03:05](https://youtu.be/xXRr9E2Caqs?t=185)
- **Momentum veto (2022)**: no position during stages 1, 3 or 4, and no entry unless the trend template passes: "you don't want to be first to the party in momentum you want to be late to the party". [How to Find and Trade Momentum Stocks, 2022-08-23, 01:32](https://youtu.be/Tl1ObCPO9m0?t=92), [How to Find and Trade Momentum Stocks, 2022-08-23, 10:39](https://youtu.be/Tl1ObCPO9m0?t=639), [How to Find and Trade Momentum Stocks, 2022-08-23, 11:10](https://youtu.be/Tl1ObCPO9m0?t=670)
- **Leverage veto (2024)**: "if you have to ask then you're not qualified to use leverage"; "most people should not be using leverage". [How to Correctly Apply Leverage in Trading, 2024-03-14, 01:01](https://youtu.be/Jxr8jrpexec?t=61), [How to Correctly Apply Leverage in Trading, 2024-03-14, 01:32](https://youtu.be/Jxr8jrpexec?t=92)

## Stops and invalidation

- **Stop placement.** The stop goes "a little bit below the cycle low… You don't want it in line with the cycle low because occasionally you get double bottoms or even quick sweeps". He uses no fixed offset ("my eye pretty much works"). For anyone who wants it mechanical he suggests ATR, standard deviations or a Parabolic SAR trail. [Answering Your Questions, 2026-08-18, 01:06](https://youtu.be/EKSjKkcIIqU?t=66), [Answering Your Questions, 2026-08-18, 02:11](https://youtu.be/EKSjKkcIIqU?t=131)
- **What counts as failure: close or wick? Versions by date.**
  - 2023: "a cycle failure occurs when the price breaks below the previous cycle low".
  - 18 Aug 2026: the trade "invalidates by failing the cycle which means closing below the cycle low".
  - 13 Oct: it "closes below wherever this cycle low confirms".
  - But on 2 Sep he declared gold's daily cycle failed "on a wicking basis" and removed the position.
  - [HOW TO TRADE CYCLES, 2023-08-30, 12:35](https://youtu.be/XWTIrduvgrU?t=755), [Answering Your Questions, 2026-08-18, 01:39](https://youtu.be/EKSjKkcIIqU?t=99), [It's Happening!, 2026-10-13, 06:18](https://youtu.be/qarjzOUTk8k?t=378), [The Funniest Analysis I've Ever Seen!, 2026-09-02, 12:51](https://youtu.be/DPhLPRVnliM?t=771)
- **A confirmed cycle low becomes the invalidation.** "Price has no business trading below that low if it's going to remain in bullish structure." For TLT: "once that low is in and confirmed, you can set that low as invalidation". In 2023 the same idea was "that becomes the line in the sand". [Let's be Brutally Honest, 2026-08-15, 09:33](https://youtu.be/9-ISGikTF4Y?t=573), [We're So Back !!, 2026-09-22, 09:18](https://youtu.be/n3R4fE6denY?t=558), [The Most Crowded Trade, 2026-10-01, 04:50](https://youtu.be/fkncsQKAIp4?t=290), [HOW TO TRADE CYCLES, 2023-08-30, 15:54](https://youtu.be/XWTIrduvgrU?t=954)
- **Do not move stops**: "not getting cute… not moving the stops around… let the trade either hit invalidation or push up into profit." [Another Record Close !!, 2026-08-08, 15:39](https://youtu.be/vSroUWCVwtU?t=939)
- **Older stop rules.**
  - 2022 momentum: the stop goes "below the lows or halfway down" the box, sized to 1–2% of the account; "don't be deterred by stops".
  - 2024: put the stop beyond the asset's normal swing. With a 7% average true range, "it is no good having a stop placed half a percent below your entry". "Find a set of local lows… put the stop just below those lows."
  - 2026-05-07: "stopped a little bit below the low as always".
  - [How to Find and Trade Momentum Stocks, 2022-08-23, 08:38](https://youtu.be/Tl1ObCPO9m0?t=518), [How to Find and Trade Momentum Stocks, 2022-08-23, 04:33](https://youtu.be/Tl1ObCPO9m0?t=273), [How to Find and Trade Momentum Stocks, 2022-08-23, 05:03](https://youtu.be/Tl1ObCPO9m0?t=303), [How to Correctly Apply Leverage in Trading, 2024-03-14, 08:12](https://youtu.be/Jxr8jrpexec?t=492), [The 4 Year Cycle is Dead - Metals to New Highs!, 2026-05-07, 01:32](https://youtu.be/z_s9A8VEXGU?t=92)
- **A breakout-retest stop goes just below the higher low.** It is tighter, so it allows more leverage at the same risk. [More Upside Ahead, 2026-08-13, 07:55](https://youtu.be/ggtv4-O6Gdw?t=475), [More Upside Ahead, 2026-08-13, 08:28](https://youtu.be/ggtv4-O6Gdw?t=508)
- **Pyramided stops**: add at the next DCL with its stop below that low, and move the first stop up to the same line. [The Treasury Just Blinked, 2026-08-20, 09:07](https://youtu.be/_Gn7BpBvA44?t=547)
- **The two accounts have different stops (17 Sep).**
  - Short-term (leveraged): hard stops always ("I need to have an area on the chart where if that level is run, I'm out").
  - Long-term (UK SIPP/ISA spot): no hard stop, because broker stops are unreliable and expire. Instead he draws a manual line under the latest confirmed DCL and cuts *all* the long-term adds the next time price trades there. The latest add gives back a little; the earlier adds keep their gains.
  - [Major Opportunity for the Long term account, 2026-09-17, 04:47](https://youtu.be/ZlYblgqzJ2w?t=287), [Major Opportunity for the Long term account, 2026-09-17, 10:30](https://youtu.be/ZlYblgqzJ2w?t=630), [Here's Why, 2026-09-18, 08:46](https://youtu.be/NUaGWQQTN5Y?t=526)
- **Drawdown-based invalidation for unstopped positions.** TLT at 20% and an average of ~84–85: a fall of 20% (to ~68) means "I'm fully wrong on this idea. Time to cut the trade" (−4% of the account). (Sizing versions are under Position sizing.) [Major Opportunity for the Long term account, 2026-09-17, 12:34](https://youtu.be/ZlYblgqzJ2w?t=754)
- **He sometimes wants to be stopped.** "We want to get stopped out. Because if we get stopped out, that sets this up as left trans and then down" (gold, 31 Aug). "I'd rather give up 2% in a short-term account so I can shove a bunch of allocation in down here." [A Massive Week Ahead, 2026-08-31, 07:54](https://youtu.be/-HDKylerUWo?t=474), [Does Sentiment Match a Bottom, 2026-08-25, 18:14](https://youtu.be/_freGHQfGEc?t=1094)
- **When an invalidation fires, flip; don't argue.** "Invalidations exist for a reason. And now it's time to flip bullish." "It's easier to pivot from bearish to bullish to back to bearish again than it is to just say, well, the market has to do this." [We're So Back !!, 2026-09-22, 13:02](https://youtu.be/n3R4fE6denY?t=782), [And No One Believes It..., 2026-10-15, 12:06](https://youtu.be/hhb_9awG53c?t=726), [And No One Believes It..., 2026-10-15, 12:38](https://youtu.be/hhb_9awG53c?t=758)
- **Invalidation for the stocks bull / blow-off call.** Early on: left translation plus failure of the current daily cycle. Then: a break of the "red legacy trendline" or failed bigger cycles. Later, the full list: "trendline breakdowns and failed daily cycles coupled with the alarm signals blowing out". Trendline rule: after new highs "i can by the rules of my own system encompass the lowest low wick here and use this as our next alarm signal". In a blow-off he sells "early on a trendline breakdown… not giving it one to two days". [Gold's Breakout is Extremely Important, 2026-08-06, 02:00](https://youtu.be/iZYEi03ZddY?t=120), [What is Going on ??, 2026-09-15, 08:13](https://youtu.be/wqa0EcxKOYY?t=493), [The Year End Everything Rally, 2026-09-26, 18:46](https://youtu.be/_gS-hSL_vOI?t=1126), [The Year End Everything Rally, 2026-09-26, 00:34](https://youtu.be/_gS-hSL_vOI?t=34), [The Next Move Will Shock Investors, 2026-09-10, 08:29](https://youtu.be/cp7MHZZBFW8?t=509)

**What brings the bear path back after the flip ("Plan B").**
- 22 Sep: a lower high followed by a left-translated, failed DCL.
- 3 Oct: "a red candle in the kind of low 70s or high 60s within the next couple of weeks".
- 8 Oct: two LT&F daily cycles in a row would make the bear squiggle "right the whole time".
- 25 Sep: "I would need to see daily cycles left translate and fail for me to actually start to think that this is going to get swept."
- 13 Oct onward: the 80% rule.

**Other theses' invalidations, by date**

| Thesis | Versions | Citation |

## Exits and targets

- **Hold winners until a technical breakdown.** After scaling in, "sit back and let the stop do the worrying", then "look for technical breakdowns to lock in the gains". "You have to be willing to give large chunks of open profit back in exchange for being able to capture the larger trends." "As long as I can be in for 70 [%] of the move that is good enough for me." [We are printing again, 2026-08-07, 03:07](https://youtu.be/ORSNkP6I4jI?t=187), [Could it be, 2026-08-22, 05:48](https://youtu.be/8ZbQVOShDco?t=348), [The Year End Everything Rally, 2026-09-26, 03:45](https://youtu.be/_gS-hSL_vOI?t=225)
- **2023 exit rules.** "Cycle highs i tend not to pay too much attention to they're unreliable." The possible exits are a swing-high pattern, trendline breakdowns, a cross back below the 10 MA, or the timing band (a straight rally to day 35–36 of a 40-day cycle means a pullback is due). His own preference is to "allow the weekly cycle to largely unfold and look for a key loss of support or a technical breakdown". [HOW TO TRADE CYCLES, 2023-08-30, 11:34](https://youtu.be/XWTIrduvgrU?t=694), [HOW TO TRADE CYCLES, 2023-08-30, 12:04](https://youtu.be/XWTIrduvgrU?t=724)
- **Pullbacks inside a trend are not exits**: after gap-ups, "a couple of red days" and then the trend resumes. [We are printing again, 2026-08-07, 02:37](https://youtu.be/ORSNkP6I4jI?t=157)
- **Equities.**
  - Push the longs into the blow-off top and sell it "or as close to it as possible", on the first trendline breakdown.
  - Go flat on the alarm gate. After yields top, get "a bit defensive or going flat" ahead of the 4-year low.
  - On 17 Oct he set a timed exit: "flat by wednesday" if HY spreads blow out and stocks decline all week.
  - [New Lows Coming For Crypto, 2026-08-11, 04:24](https://youtu.be/UcAX4VpRTfg?t=264), [The Next Move Will Shock Investors, 2026-09-10, 08:29](https://youtu.be/cp7MHZZBFW8?t=509), [Here's my Trading Plan for the Week, 2026-10-05, 01:35](https://youtu.be/fMQyp-wqH8Y?t=95), [This Changes Things, 2026-10-17, 14:20](https://youtu.be/l4d4MOUG1Gg?t=860)
- **Equity daily-cycle map (13 Oct)**: a top around day 30–35, then the pullback into the day-40 low. [It's Happening!, 2026-10-13, 01:01](https://youtu.be/qarjzOUTk8k?t=61)
- **The hedge exit.** Cover the BTC short (from just below 80K) at the 4-year low and "hop out of that hedge and into the spot bitcoin market", roughly doubling the stack. After 83K broke this hedge is not mentioned again (Parts 4–5). [Let's be Brutally Honest, 2026-08-15, 08:29](https://youtu.be/9-ISGikTF4Y?t=509), [Let's be Brutally Honest, 2026-08-15, 24:26](https://youtu.be/9-ISGikTF4Y?t=1466)
- **BTC short-term long after the flip**: if the DCL rally makes new highs, "we'll profit from that and then try to duck out before the decline into the weekly cycle low". [Here's What's Next !!, 2026-10-09, 14:01](https://youtu.be/9FrE_d7KaFc?t=841)
- **Take the net gain when a re-entry fails.** The Dow trade went from ~3R open to giving back 1R, and he cut it "so I can still walk away net green". [Major Opportunity for the Long term account, 2026-09-17, 03:11](https://youtu.be/ZlYblgqzJ2w?t=191), [Major Opportunity for the Long term account, 2026-09-17, 06:20](https://youtu.be/ZlYblgqzJ2w?t=380)
- **Targets are R-multiples off a stop at the cycle low**: ETH "10R"; XRP "a 50% stop… a 15R trade"; semis "at least four to one"; TLT "approximately a double". [FOMC Arrives! Here's How I am Trading it, 2026-07-29, 08:45](https://youtu.be/nKpG-HTBRrQ?t=525), [FOMC Arrives! Here's How I am Trading it, 2026-07-29, 10:16](https://youtu.be/nKpG-HTBRrQ?t=616), [The Lows Finally Confirmed !!, 2026-07-31, 07:52](https://youtu.be/bkf_ALW8W_A?t=472), [Gold at Critical Inflection Point !!, 2026-08-10, 11:43](https://youtu.be/MMnkrbLKZRc?t=703)
- **Measured moves and retracements.** A BTC break of 82–83K gives ~118K. After a big straight-line push, "a nice 50% retracement". A parabolic advance that breaks retraces "70-ish percent". An S&P rectangle break targets "at least 8k". [Does Sentiment Match a Bottom, 2026-08-25, 09:57](https://youtu.be/_freGHQfGEc?t=597), [The Most Important Level In Finance, 2026-08-26, 08:14](https://youtu.be/c46MwOOnips?t=494), [We've Reached a Major Inflection Point, 2026-09-30, 02:05](https://youtu.be/O2PXnNlV3gA?t=125), [It's Looking Good!, 2026-10-06, 03:48](https://youtu.be/un3YVNZSEVQ?t=228)
- **Gold-cycle playbook**: buy the underlying from the lows "to about halfway", then add the miners, "because the miners tend to lag the initial part of the rally and then catch up". [It's Happening!, 2026-10-13, 02:36](https://youtu.be/qarjzOUTk8k?t=156)
- **Older and extra exit rules.**
  - 2024: for the BTC cycle top, "we'll sell the trend line breakdown" on the "third line of attack". With a right-translated top he would "count the number of weeks" to the due 4-year low and "use the trend lines as Confluence".
  - 2025: "a big rip roaring rally… And then a big trend line breakdown and then camel can say, 'Oh, there's my exit signal.'" If price only ranges, he lightens up in Q4.
  - 8 Sep 2026: sell "trendline breakdowns up here towards Christmas". "I like to empty the portfolio a little bit, take some gains off the table" before his Christmas break.
  - 7 May 2026: took profit on a leverage position at "about 5.3 R" and kept the long-term adds. His framing: "You risk a little bit to make a lot."
  - 2022 momentum: keep buying each new box breakout as the stock "forms a base and then moves up".
  - [Principles for staying ahead in investing, 2024-01-30, 08:07](https://youtu.be/4wc4EOj6KOo?t=487), [Principles for staying ahead in investing, 2024-01-30, 14:46](https://youtu.be/4wc4EOj6KOo?t=886), [The 4 Year Cycle is 95% Complete..., 2025-08-27, 09:11](https://youtu.be/9-JtVbT-4-o?t=551), [The 4 Year Cycle is 95% Complete..., 2025-08-27, 19:53](https://youtu.be/9-JtVbT-4-o?t=1193), [The Bitcoin Bart is Still Valid, 2026-09-08, 09:26](https://youtu.be/fDMJEhj2CGo?t=566), [The Bitcoin Bart is Still Valid, 2026-09-08, 10:00](https://youtu.be/fDMJEhj2CGo?t=600), [The 4 Year Cycle is Dead - Metals to New Highs!, 2026-05-07, 08:41](https://youtu.be/z_s9A8VEXGU?t=521), [The 4 Year Cycle is Dead - Metals to New Highs!, 2026-05-07, 01:32](https://youtu.be/z_s9A8VEXGU?t=92), [How to Find and Trade Momentum Stocks, 2022-08-23, 04:33](https://youtu.be/Tl1ObCPO9m0?t=273)
- **Long-term holds**: metals to "2030, if not 2033"; TLT to ~160; alts in "small tranches… for a year to three years". He calls the long-range numbers "worthless guess[es]". [FOMC Shakeout / Trap & GO !!, 2026-09-16, 10:26](https://youtu.be/28wXrOWuVyA?t=626), [Simulation Confirmed, Narrative Arrives, 2026-09-14, 08:55](https://youtu.be/LK5YLwkwCiI?t=535), [More Upside Ahead, 2026-08-13, 10:38](https://youtu.be/ggtv4-O6Gdw?t=638), [This Changes Things, 2026-10-17, 19:09](https://youtu.be/l4d4MOUG1Gg?t=1149)

## Position sizing and risk

- **Risk per trade, by date.** In 2023: stops of "one to two percent of your overall account"; "when you're wrong you're wrong in a small way". From August 2026: 2%. "Put the stop where it needs to go. And then look at how much you're going to lose… If you're going to lose more than 2%. You can't afford to take the trade. Or you have to size it down." Leverage is the sizing dial, not a conviction meter: a 0.2% unlevered stop allows a "10x leverage equivalent". [HOW TO TRADE CYCLES, 2023-08-30, 02:02](https://youtu.be/XWTIrduvgrU?t=122), [Answering Your Questions, 2026-08-18, 15:12](https://youtu.be/EKSjKkcIIqU?t=912), [Answering Your Questions, 2026-08-18, 15:44](https://youtu.be/EKSjKkcIIqU?t=944), [Answering Your Questions, 2026-08-18, 14:42](https://youtu.be/EKSjKkcIIqU?t=882)
- **The 2024 risk video: 2% per trade and 6% per sector is the whole edge.**
  - "2% maximum risk on a stoploss": on a $10,000 account, "just $200".
  - "A maximum of 6% across the same sector", because past ~6% the recovery maths turn against you. His table: −10% needs +11%, −20% needs +25%, −50% needs +100%, −90% needs +900%.
  - No exceptions for small accounts, hot streaks or losing streaks ("each trade is independent of the prior one").
  - "TA is essentially worthless" without this.
  - [How to Manage Risk in Trading, 2024-03-12, 06:11](https://youtu.be/yuKry_fKC5I?t=371), [How to Manage Risk in Trading, 2024-03-12, 06:41](https://youtu.be/yuKry_fKC5I?t=401), [How to Manage Risk in Trading, 2024-03-12, 07:12](https://youtu.be/yuKry_fKC5I?t=432), [How to Manage Risk in Trading, 2024-03-12, 07:44](https://youtu.be/yuKry_fKC5I?t=464), [How to Manage Risk in Trading, 2024-03-12, 09:17](https://youtu.be/yuKry_fKC5I?t=557), [How to Manage Risk in Trading, 2024-03-12, 11:50](https://youtu.be/yuKry_fKC5I?t=710), [How to Manage Risk in Trading, 2024-03-12, 02:34](https://youtu.be/yuKry_fKC5I?t=154)
- **The 2024 leverage video: leverage is a by-product of the stop.** This is the same rule as his 18 Aug 2026 Q&A.
  - Step 1: put the stop where it must go. Step 2: compute the loss if it is hit. Step 3: add leverage only while that loss stays within "1 to 2%" of the account.
  - "2x leverage can be degenerate", while "120x… could be considered very safe". He "would not recommend anyone actually use leverage".
  - [How to Correctly Apply Leverage in Trading, 2024-03-14, 02:03](https://youtu.be/Jxr8jrpexec?t=123), [How to Correctly Apply Leverage in Trading, 2024-03-14, 04:37](https://youtu.be/Jxr8jrpexec?t=277), [How to Correctly Apply Leverage in Trading, 2024-03-14, 07:40](https://youtu.be/Jxr8jrpexec?t=460), [How to Correctly Apply Leverage in Trading, 2024-03-14, 08:42](https://youtu.be/Jxr8jrpexec?t=522), [How to Correctly Apply Leverage in Trading, 2024-03-14, 01:32](https://youtu.be/Jxr8jrpexec?t=92)
- **The 70% CAGR benchmark (2024; 2026-04-11).**
  - 2024: $5,000 at 70% a year is "$4.5 million" in a decade, and 45% a year on $10,000 is "very doable".
  - 2026-04-11: the trading book should aim for ~70% a year ("Four and a half percent ish on a monthly basis"). Otherwise ask "would I be better off just holding Bitcoin?", since BTC has ~70% CAGR. A loss above 2% on one trade, or above 6% in a sector, means "you suck at trading".
  - Worked spread-bet example (2026-04-11): £1 a point on the S&P with a 100-point stop = £100 = 2% of a £5,000 account. That trade's +330 points in ~32 days was ≈ 6.6% of the account. He builds exposure gradually rather than going straight to the 6% sector cap.
  - [How to Manage Risk in Trading, 2024-03-12, 13:55](https://youtu.be/yuKry_fKC5I?t=835), [How to Manage Risk in Trading, 2024-03-12, 14:26](https://youtu.be/yuKry_fKC5I?t=866), [FREE VIDEO: 70% CAGR, Risk management & Mathematical example, 2026-04-11, 00:31](https://youtu.be/F7gm9z5G3lw?t=31), [FREE VIDEO: 70% CAGR, Risk management & Mathematical example, 2026-04-11, 05:05](https://youtu.be/F7gm9z5G3lw?t=305), [FREE VIDEO: 70% CAGR, Risk management & Mathematical example, 2026-04-11, 04:03](https://youtu.be/F7gm9z5G3lw?t=243), [FREE VIDEO: 70% CAGR, Risk management & Mathematical example, 2026-04-11, 07:08](https://youtu.be/F7gm9z5G3lw?t=428), [FREE VIDEO: 70% CAGR, Risk management & Mathematical example, 2026-04-11, 08:10](https://youtu.be/F7gm9z5G3lw?t=490), [FREE VIDEO: 70% CAGR, Risk management & Mathematical example, 2026-04-11, 10:44](https://youtu.be/F7gm9z5G3lw?t=644)
- **Core position plus swings (2024)**: "I try to get in at the fouryear cycle low and get out at the top", making swing trades on the way up. [Cycle Trading with Andrew (@GoldnGuitars), 2024-08-10, 35:55](https://youtu.be/KSum66r_w8s?t=2155)
- **Short-term account: fixed 2%, never varied** "depending on conviction or market conditions or anything else". His worked example: three 2% stops followed by one 5R winner leaves +4%. Halving the risk after the losses would leave −1%. Sector risk is capped at 6% "once I've earned the right to have it". [Major Opportunity for the Long term account, 2026-09-17, 03:11](https://youtu.be/ZlYblgqzJ2w?t=191), [Major Opportunity for the Long term account, 2026-09-17, 03:43](https://youtu.be/ZlYblgqzJ2w?t=223), [Major Opportunity for the Long term account, 2026-09-17, 08:55](https://youtu.be/ZlYblgqzJ2w?t=535)
- **Long-term account: "absolute position sizing and assuming it can go to zero".** Each position is a 5% clip. He adds another 5% at the next confirmed DCL only once the earlier clip is in profit. Tolerable drawdown is 6%, "even 10%". Size is scaled by volatility: no 20% MSTR position in one or two clips, as was done with TLT. Without a stop: allocate 2% so that a go-to-zero costs 2%, or allocate 4% and cut at a 50% drawdown. [Major Opportunity for the Long term account, 2026-09-17, 08:24](https://youtu.be/ZlYblgqzJ2w?t=504), [Major Opportunity for the Long term account, 2026-09-17, 09:26](https://youtu.be/ZlYblgqzJ2w?t=566), [Major Opportunity for the Long term account, 2026-09-17, 12:34](https://youtu.be/ZlYblgqzJ2w?t=754), [Answering Your Questions, 2026-08-18, 06:18](https://youtu.be/EKSjKkcIIqU?t=378)
- **Scale in, never all-in.** "This is how we let the initial trades finance risk on additional trades." Past stacks he cites: 4–5 MSTR positions, 3–4 BTC, 3 SILJ. Small first, and "once I have won the right to trade bigger" add more. [The Treasury Just Blinked, 2026-08-20, 06:28](https://youtu.be/_Gn7BpBvA44?t=388), [The Treasury Just Blinked, 2026-08-20, 09:40](https://youtu.be/_Gn7BpBvA44?t=580), [It's happening, 2026-09-12, 22:30](https://youtu.be/ZpYL6r2JzuY?t=1350)
- **Cycle-level allocation.** Mostly cash through 2026, going "largely fully exposed" at the 4-year lows. At the BTC low, "double the Bitcoin stack" and add higher-beta names (MSTR, Circle). In October he planned to buy spot in thirds ("smash buying" in the high 60s / low 70s), taking a small DCL long first and the heavy buying at the weekly cycle low. [I'm Sure About This, 2026-07-25, 01:14](https://youtu.be/Mm5NUZElP-Y?t=74), [It's starting, 2026-08-01, 02:06](https://youtu.be/L3QWk203NR4?t=126), [A Bullish Q4 is Loading !!, 2026-10-02, 07:30](https://youtu.be/MG8WI9O0Eyk?t=450), [This Changes Things, 2026-10-17, 21:44](https://youtu.be/l4d4MOUG1Gg?t=1304), [The Next Leg Higher has Started!, 2026-10-03, 04:16](https://youtu.be/IYfTEZtA2f8?t=256)
- **TLT sizing arithmetic, by date (the worst case keeps moving).**
  - 20 Aug: 20% of the long-term account at an average of ~85; a 20% fall costs 4% of the account, a win adds 20% "to my entire liquid portfolio".
  - 11 Sep: 20% of the *liquid portfolio*; a low at 75 is "a 10%-ish loss… which of course is 2%".
  - 17 Sep: 76 costs 2%; ~68 means "fully wrong", −4%.
  - 9 Oct: "risking about 2% to 4%".
  - [The Treasury Just Blinked, 2026-08-20, 19:46](https://youtu.be/_Gn7BpBvA44?t=1186), [The Treasury Just Blinked, 2026-08-20, 21:26](https://youtu.be/_Gn7BpBvA44?t=1286), [Investors are Quitting at the Worst Time, 2026-09-11, 03:40](https://youtu.be/V55qMPLctPE?t=220), [Investors are Quitting at the Worst Time, 2026-09-11, 13:48](https://youtu.be/V55qMPLctPE?t=828), [Major Opportunity for the Long term account, 2026-09-17, 12:34](https://youtu.be/ZlYblgqzJ2w?t=754), [Here's What's Next !!, 2026-10-09, 04:11](https://youtu.be/9FrE_d7KaFc?t=251)
- **Metals allocation clock: a "20 to 25% core allocation", deadline restated.**
  - 25 Jul: "three to six months away" from fully exposed.
  - 11 Aug: 1.5 to 6 weeks.
  - 17 Aug: "within the next five or so trading sessions".
  - 20 and 22 Aug: within six weeks.
  - 25 Aug: "next five weeks or so".
  - 27 Aug: "four to five weeks… no more than that".
  - 1 Sep: "pretty much fully allocated" by ~21 Sep.
  - 18 Sep: actually ~4% of the long-term account.
  - October: nothing until the weekly cycle low.

## Timeframes

- **Each low is confirmed by a close on its own timeframe**: daily for DCLs, weekly for weekly lows, monthly for 4-year lows. "It's the weekly close that matters. And more importantly than the weekly closes, it's the monthly." [Does Sentiment Match a Bottom, 2026-08-25, 11:30](https://youtu.be/_freGHQfGEc?t=690), [It's starting, 2026-08-01, 13:22](https://youtu.be/L3QWk203NR4?t=802)
- **The two books.**
  - Short-term account: leveraged derivatives and CFDs, trading daily cycles in indices, metals and BTC.
  - Long-term account: spot in a UK SIPP/ISA, holding TLT, semis, metals and miners, and trading weekly, yearly and 4-year lows.
  - [Major Opportunity for the Long term account, 2026-09-17, 00:33](https://youtu.be/ZlYblgqzJ2w?t=33), [Answering Your Questions, 2026-08-18, 14:12](https://youtu.be/EKSjKkcIIqU?t=852), [It's starting, 2026-08-01, 14:23](https://youtu.be/L3QWk203NR4?t=863)
- **How days are counted.** Equity daily cycles are counted in **trading days**. He corrects himself on air: "This is weekend candles. Let me go back here". BTC is counted in calendar days. In September his BTC day counts imply day 0 = 1 Aug, while the ledger anchored the count at the 16 Aug low. This is not reconciled on air. [The Most Crowded Trade, 2026-10-01, 12:26](https://youtu.be/fkncsQKAIp4?t=746), [It's Happening Again !! (Asset Cycles Aligning), 2026-09-07, 04:11](https://youtu.be/OqzFvsbmfGE?t=251), [Simulation Confirmed, Narrative Arrives, 2026-09-14, 13:08](https://youtu.be/LK5YLwkwCiI?t=788)
- **Chart conventions.** Linear, not log, charts; TVC:GOLD rather than OANDA. TLT's 7.5-year cycle is read on a 3-month chart. A 3-day BTC chart was set against a 1-day 2022 chart to compare the structure. [Rejected at Resistance, 2026-08-14, 04:06](https://youtu.be/xXRr9E2Caqs?t=246), [The Most Crowded Trade, 2026-10-01, 02:44](https://youtu.be/fkncsQKAIp4?t=164), [Does Sentiment Match a Bottom, 2026-08-25, 02:39](https://youtu.be/_freGHQfGEc?t=159)
- **Swing beats day trading** (SPY's gains come outside regular hours). [We're So Back !!, 2026-09-22, 07:48](https://youtu.be/n3R4fE6denY?t=468)
- **Older timeframe notes.** 2022: the momentum trend template uses daily MAs and the boxes are drawn on the weekly chart. 2026-04-11: "wait approximately every 40 days and buy a cycle low in the S&P 500… for Bitcoin, wait every 60 days", executed by UK spread betting. [How to Find and Trade Momentum Stocks, 2022-08-23, 08:07](https://youtu.be/Tl1ObCPO9m0?t=487), [FREE VIDEO: 70% CAGR, Risk management & Mathematical example, 2026-04-11, 02:02](https://youtu.be/F7gm9z5G3lw?t=122), [FREE VIDEO: 70% CAGR, Risk management & Mathematical example, 2026-04-11, 05:36](https://youtu.be/F7gm9z5G3lw?t=336)

- **"Tell that to the bond market" ("Camel's razor").** "Whenever this number reads 85… whichever side the 85% is on is what we will be seeing." The rule is applied: 92% for a hike on 15 Sep, the hike on 16 Sep, then "one and done". "It is the bond market in aggregate that sets rates, not the Fed." He does not listen to FOMC press conferences. [Another FOMC Shakeout, 2026-07-28, 03:40](https://youtu.be/wyoC1nHivA8?t=220), [What is Going on ??, 2026-09-15, 00:00](https://youtu.be/wqa0EcxKOYY?t=0), [Investors are Quitting at the Worst Time, 2026-09-11, 12:43](https://youtu.be/V55qMPLctPE?t=763), [The Next Leg Higher has Started!, 2026-10-03, 11:54](https://youtu.be/IYfTEZtA2f8?t=714), [Major Opportunity for the Long term account, 2026-09-17, 02:08](https://youtu.be/ZlYblgqzJ2w?t=128)
- **Background theses: these justify positions but are not triggers.**
  - Disinflation turning to brief deflation. Evidence cited: CPI 4.2 → 3.5, Truflation, 1-year swaps under 2%, trimmed-mean PCE, and core CPI ex-shelter (ALNRI) at 0.8%.
  - A labour market in a "lead-in before a pop". Unemployment 4.1–4.4; more than 2M jobs revised out over three years; the Sahm rule rolling over. He expects the NBER to backdate a recession over the coming crash.
  - Stealth QE and Treasury buybacks as yield-curve control.
  - Margin debt/GDP at a new high, read as "major top territory".
  - [I'm Sure About This, 2026-07-25, 05:56](https://youtu.be/Mm5NUZElP-Y?t=356), [It's starting, 2026-08-01, 06:12](https://youtu.be/L3QWk203NR4?t=372), [And No One Believes It..., 2026-10-15, 03:38](https://youtu.be/hhb_9awG53c?t=218), [I'm Sure About This, 2026-07-25, 09:11](https://youtu.be/Mm5NUZElP-Y?t=551), [It's happening, 2026-09-12, 13:22](https://youtu.be/ZpYL6r2JzuY?t=802), [This Changes Things, 2026-10-17, 12:11](https://youtu.be/l4d4MOUG1Gg?t=731), [It's a trap, 2026-07-27, 09:47](https://youtu.be/C3E_zU5a6c4?t=587), [More Upside Ahead, 2026-08-13, 04:14](https://youtu.be/ggtv4-O6Gdw?t=254)
- **The 2025 end-game thesis.** Valuations are at records (Buffett indicator 217%). At the late-2026 lows he expects rates "at or near zero" and "a late and reluctant Fed to start outright QE", followed by "a lost decade of stagflation". [The 4 Year Cycle is 95% Complete..., 2025-08-27, 11:43](https://youtu.be/9-JtVbT-4-o?t=703), [The 4 Year Cycle is 95% Complete..., 2025-08-27, 16:51](https://youtu.be/9-JtVbT-4-o?t=1011), [The 4 Year Cycle is 95% Complete..., 2025-08-27, 17:52](https://youtu.be/9-JtVbT-4-o?t=1072)
- **"Cycles are in control. Everything else is narrative."** He frames bank custody, a US strategic reserve and similar news as the narrative for the *next* BTC 4-year cycle. [New Lows Coming For Crypto, 2026-08-11, 04:57](https://youtu.be/UcAX4VpRTfg?t=297), [Record Shorts, 2026-08-19, 01:30](https://youtu.be/O2e2PpZn6vA?t=90)

## Indicator settings stated (exact numbers)

**No oscillator lengths are spoken in any of the 66 transcripts or in the 2023 tutorial.** He uses DSS Bressert only for crosses: a bullish cross "down the bottom" (out of oversold) confirms a low on the daily, weekly or monthly, and a "divergent higher low… on the monthly" is a stronger confirmation. The ledger's (10, 9, 5, 80/20) comes from a screen frame, not speech. The TSI is offered as an alternative (31 Aug). [Major Opportunity for the Long term account, 2026-09-17, 17:52](https://youtu.be/ZlYblgqzJ2w?t=1072), [The Treasury Just Blinked, 2026-08-20, 20:55](https://youtu.be/_Gn7BpBvA44?t=1255), [A Massive Week Ahead, 2026-08-31, 05:19](https://youtu.be/-HDKylerUWo?t=319)

Moving averages and other tools:
- 10 SMA on the timeframe of the cycle being confirmed. In 2023 it was "the 10 moving average… what bob lucas likes to use".
- 200-day MA (gold breakout).
- BTC 50-week and 100-week MAs, the 50/200 golden cross, and the 200-week MA.
- Parabolic SAR (a flip "helps"; also a trailing-stop option).
- Quoted only: a Supertrend at 79.5K, and TD Sequential 9.
- 2022: 50-, 150- and 200-day SMAs for the momentum trend template. 2026-09-25 (quoted): the 30-week MA for stage analysis.

**Building the windows by hand (18 Aug).** Mark the obvious monthly pivots and confirm them with Bressert crosses. Then nest weekly and daily cycles, using arrows of different colours. Do this "for the entire price history of the asset… and then average them all out at the end and then apply a 10% tolerance. That will give you your counts." TradingView cyclic lines or timing lines anchored low to low project equidistant lows, but "typically one throws the whole thing out". [Answering Your Questions, 2026-08-18, 07:20](https://youtu.be/EKSjKkcIIqU?t=440), [Answering Your Questions, 2026-08-18, 08:20](https://youtu.be/EKSjKkcIIqU?t=500), [Answering Your Questions, 2026-08-18, 09:56](https://youtu.be/EKSjKkcIIqU?t=596)

Other thresholds:
- FedWatch 85% = "certain".
- Sentiment-poll extremes at 60–65% on one side (the GFC record was 70/19/11).

[FOMC Arrives! Here's How I am Trading it, 2026-07-29, 01:01](https://youtu.be/nKpG-HTBRrQ?t=61), [And I Can Prove It !!, 2026-09-03, 01:41](https://youtu.be/aK_JCKqbMJQ?t=101)

## Calibration numbers and falsifiable side-claims

- **How many cycle lows fall inside the window, by date.** 2023: "80% of the time". 27 Aug 2025: "70 to 80% of the cycle lows show up inside the expected window… 20 to 30% might fall slightly outside… say plus or minus a weekly cycle", and "That's what the edge is entirely structured around". 6 Aug 2026: "70 to 80%… gold would definitely be up towards that 80%". 10 Sep 2026: "70 to 80% of all lows… depending on the asset class", with 20–30% "slightly early". He also said in 2023 that cycles "work best in trending markets". [HOW TO TRADE CYCLES, 2023-08-30, 05:16](https://youtu.be/XWTIrduvgrU?t=316), [The 4 Year Cycle is 95% Complete..., 2025-08-27, 07:37](https://youtu.be/9-JtVbT-4-o?t=457), [The 4 Year Cycle is 95% Complete..., 2025-08-27, 08:39](https://youtu.be/9-JtVbT-4-o?t=519), [Gold's Breakout is Extremely Important, 2026-08-06, 05:44](https://youtu.be/iZYEi03ZddY?t=344), [The Next Move Will Shock Investors, 2026-09-10, 15:00](https://youtu.be/cp7MHZZBFW8?t=900)
- **The 80/80 DCL resolution rule** (13 Oct). He also puts 60–70% on a 10y weekly cycle low that left-translates and fails (1 Oct). [It's Happening!, 2026-10-13, 06:18](https://youtu.be/qarjzOUTk8k?t=378), [The Most Crowded Trade, 2026-10-01, 01:39](https://youtu.be/fkncsQKAIp4?t=99)
- **"Timing-based consolidations"** (a time-based low followed by a breakout) are rare: "I can think of two or three" in more than four years. [Simulation Confirmed, Narrative Arrives, 2026-09-14, 14:42](https://youtu.be/LK5YLwkwCiI?t=882), [Simulation Confirmed, Narrative Arrives, 2026-09-14, 15:15](https://youtu.be/LK5YLwkwCiI?t=915)
- **BTC bear market.** It has used "84% of that average time"; it was "only about 50% top to bottom" and took "about a half to two thirds of the time"; "the shortest bear market in both duration and drawdown"; "the first four-year cycle it's ever thrown this early". [Bitcoin Bulls Got Real Quiet All of a Sudden..., 2026-09-01, 02:36](https://youtu.be/n3XxAlp79oM?t=156), [Does Sentiment Match a Bottom, 2026-08-25, 10:29](https://youtu.be/_freGHQfGEc?t=629), [We're So Back, 2026-09-09, 04:07](https://youtu.be/O_zc6fm1I4g?t=247), [The Year End Everything Rally, 2026-09-26, 02:40](https://youtu.be/_gS-hSL_vOI?t=160)
- **BTC moving-average facts.** Above its 200-week MA "92% of the time" (Saylor). The weekly RSI bullish divergence ran 203 days into the last 4-year window. [It's Make or Break, 2026-08-03, 04:57](https://youtu.be/jweSBXongzE?t=297), [It's a trap, 2026-07-27, 03:03](https://youtu.be/C3E_zU5a6c4?t=183)
- **The NDX statistic (quoted).** After a 10% pullback, a return to within 5% of the ATH has led to higher prices 72/81/88/97% of the time at 2 weeks / 2 months / 3 months / 6 months. "100% of these… occurred within the context of secular bull markets." [We are printing again, 2026-08-07, 01:34](https://youtu.be/ORSNkP6I4jI?t=94)
- **S&P Q4 seasonality (quoted).** With YTD +10–20% going into Q4, Q4 was higher "85% of cases and up 5.3% on average". [A Bullish Q4 is Loading !!, 2026-10-02, 03:47](https://youtu.be/MG8WI9O0Eyk?t=227)
