## Guest: Andrew (@GoldnGuitars), 2024-08-10 collab. These are his rules, not Camel's

- **The back-test of a fresh low.** After any cycle low there is "going to be a back test of that low", typically between bars 5 and 10: months 5–10 on the monthly chart, days 5–10 on the daily, weeks 5–10 on the weekly. [Cycle Trading with Andrew (@GoldnGuitars), 2024-08-10, 02:06](https://youtu.be/KSum66r_w8s?t=126)
- **Typical lengths.** Weekly cycles run "around 30 weeks", with "32 to 34" typical in that case. BTC daily cycles run 50–60 days, with "four or maybe five" dailies before an intermediate (weekly) cycle low. The SPX daily cycle is "around 37 38 39 40 days". GDX monthly cycles run 27–32 months. Oil weekly cycles average ~70 weeks. [Cycle Trading with Andrew (@GoldnGuitars), 2024-08-10, 02:37](https://youtu.be/KSum66r_w8s?t=157), [Cycle Trading with Andrew (@GoldnGuitars), 2024-08-10, 26:04](https://youtu.be/KSum66r_w8s?t=1564), [Cycle Trading with Andrew (@GoldnGuitars), 2024-08-10, 20:31](https://youtu.be/KSum66r_w8s?t=1231), [Cycle Trading with Andrew (@GoldnGuitars), 2024-08-10, 16:26](https://youtu.be/KSum66r_w8s?t=986), [Cycle Trading with Andrew (@GoldnGuitars), 2024-08-10, 39:59](https://youtu.be/KSum66r_w8s?t=2399)
- **Three confirmations of an intermediate cycle low**: a swing low that closes "above the entirety and Wick of that candle", a close above the MA10, and a break of the declining trendline. Bullish divergence after an undercut makes him "more bullish not less". [Cycle Trading with Andrew (@GoldnGuitars), 2024-08-10, 25:03](https://youtu.be/KSum66r_w8s?t=1503), [Cycle Trading with Andrew (@GoldnGuitars), 2024-08-10, 25:34](https://youtu.be/KSum66r_w8s?t=1534)
- **Counting weekly cycles with internal halves.** Half-cycle high, half-cycle low, weekly-cycle high, then the weekly-cycle low, "with a break of the trend line being the confirmation"; this allows weekly cycles into the 40s of weeks. He credits the idea to Arlovsky. "The correct way to do it is the way that the market decides." [Cycle Trading with Andrew (@GoldnGuitars), 2024-08-10, 04:09](https://youtu.be/KSum66r_w8s?t=249), [Cycle Trading with Andrew (@GoldnGuitars), 2024-08-10, 05:11](https://youtu.be/KSum66r_w8s?t=311)
- **Inversions cluster around intermediate cycle lows**: "coming into your ICL and coming out of your ICL are often areas when it happens". A failed daily cycle can resolve into a weekly half-cycle low and then go higher, "so you can't just blindly short". [Cycle Trading with Andrew (@GoldnGuitars), 2024-08-10, 32:47](https://youtu.be/KSum66r_w8s?t=1967), [Cycle Trading with Andrew (@GoldnGuitars), 2024-08-10, 28:06](https://youtu.be/KSum66r_w8s?t=1686)
- **Trust the higher timeframe**: "it's always more important to trust your higher frame counts". "That's why it's important to buy your… major lows and even… your intermediate cycle lows." [Cycle Trading with Andrew (@GoldnGuitars), 2024-08-10, 24:03](https://youtu.be/KSum66r_w8s?t=1443), [Cycle Trading with Andrew (@GoldnGuitars), 2024-08-10, 55:09](https://youtu.be/KSum66r_w8s?t=3309)
- **Lower-timeframe triggers.** On US100/US500 he uses a 4-hour cycle of "about 50 hours give or take" and enters on a trendline break inside its timing band, trading TQQQ. On BTC he uses a 12-hour chart where the next low comes in "50 to 60 bars". [Cycle Trading with Andrew (@GoldnGuitars), 2024-08-10, 51:33](https://youtu.be/KSum66r_w8s?t=3093), [Cycle Trading with Andrew (@GoldnGuitars), 2024-08-10, 52:03](https://youtu.be/KSum66r_w8s?t=3123), [Cycle Trading with Andrew (@GoldnGuitars), 2024-08-10, 54:06](https://youtu.be/KSum66r_w8s?t=3246)
- **Other tools**: the TSI for oversold, Andrew's Pitchforks, a 17-year top-to-top equity cycle and the GLD/SPX ratio. All are retrospective. [Cycle Trading with Andrew (@GoldnGuitars), 2024-08-10, 05:42](https://youtu.be/KSum66r_w8s?t=342), [Cycle Trading with Andrew (@GoldnGuitars), 2024-08-10, 37:25](https://youtu.be/KSum66r_w8s?t=2245), [Cycle Trading with Andrew (@GoldnGuitars), 2024-08-10, 01:02](https://youtu.be/KSum66r_w8s?t=62)

## What his indicator computes (CF Cycle Trading Indicator / "Cipher C")

**Sources.** There are two kinds, kept apart below.
- **His videos.** All 78 transcripts were searched (the 11 added later were read in full) for "indicator", "confluence", "arrow", "cipher/cypher", "signal", "cftrading", "backtest", "dashboard", "settings", "optimi[sz]e", "paint" and "colour", and each passage was read in context.
- **The vendor's public pages**: the current GitBook "features deep dive" (core and additional features), the archived 1.0 docs (under `archive-not-visible/legacy-archive`, cited as "1.0") and the cftrading.co.uk sales page.

The docs are marketing copy. Every number in them is the vendor's claim and none has been verified. The indicator was built "between Camel Finance and the developers at Variance Technologies", who "quantified the intricate timing windows and rule sets that define camel cycle low analysis". The introduction calls it "Camel Finance's manual process" turned "into a rules-based system". [CF docs 1.0: How We Built It](https://camelfinance.gitbook.io/camel-finance-features-deep-dive/archive-not-visible/legacy-archive/cf-cycle-trading-indicator-1.0/overview-1.1/how-we-built-it.md), [CF docs: Introduction](https://camelfinance.gitbook.io/camel-finance-features-deep-dive/cf-cycle-trading-indicator/introduction.md)

**Names.**
- On air he calls it "my indicator", "the indicator", "the camel indicator", "the Camel Finance indicator" (ASR "Campbell Finance"), "a cycle trading indicator" and "a trading indicator".
- The vendor's name is "CF Cycle Trading Indicator", a TradingView script.
- **"Cipher C" appears in neither the videos nor the docs.** The only "Cipher" in the 78 transcripts is Cipher Mining (14 Oct).

[Does Sentiment Match a Bottom, 2026-08-25, 13:37](https://youtu.be/_freGHQfGEc?t=817), [A Massive Week Ahead, 2026-08-31, 05:19](https://youtu.be/-HDKylerUWo?t=319), [How I outperformed Bitcoin last cycle, 2026-10-14, 04:45](https://youtu.be/52ZlNcndcSE?t=285), [CF docs: Introduction](https://camelfinance.gitbook.io/camel-finance-features-deep-dive/cf-cycle-trading-indicator/introduction.md)

### What it outputs, feature by feature

| Feature | What the docs state | What the videos add | Sources |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Cycle-low detection: DCL, WCL, YCL** | It "Detects Daily, Weekly, and Yearly Cycle Lows", each drawn as an icon plus a line. A YCL "typically occurs every few years". 1.0 claims "70-80% accuracy". Fine-tuned behaviour needs the **Daily (1D) timeframe**, "required for full indicator functionality", and 1.0 says it is "optimised for use on the daily timeframe". | It labels lows "in real time" (DXY weekly, metals, MSFT, the Dow). It also labels inversions ("picked this as an inversion"; "nailed this as an inversion… running a new count"). **The docs never mention inversions.** | [CF docs: Daily Cycle Lows](https://camelfinance.gitbook.io/camel-finance-features-deep-dive/cf-cycle-trading-indicator/core-features/daily-cycle-lows.md), [CF docs 1.0: Cycle Detection](https://camelfinance.gitbook.io/camel-finance-features-deep-dive/archive-not-visible/legacy-archive/cf-cycle-trading-indicator-1.0/key-features-1.2/cycle-detection.md), [CF docs: Finetuned Asset Detection](https://camelfinance.gitbook.io/camel-finance-features-deep-dive/cf-cycle-trading-indicator/additional-features/finetuned-asset-detection.md), [CF docs 1.0: Multi-Asset](https://camelfinance.gitbook.io/camel-finance-features-deep-dive/archive-not-visible/legacy-archive/cf-cycle-trading-indicator-1.0/key-features-1.2/multi-asset-capability.md), [What is Going on ??, 2026-09-15, 07:43](https://youtu.be/wqa0EcxKOYY?t=463), [Major Opportunity for the Long term account, 2026-09-17, 19:27](https://youtu.be/ZlYblgqzJ2w?t=1167), [The Treasury Just Blinked, 2026-08-20, 15:30](https://youtu.be/_Gn7BpBvA44?t=930) |
| **Confirmation states (DCL)** | Four icons. ✅ Confirmed: "Shows a ✅ icon the moment all rules align." 🕓 Unconfirmed: "provisional… low probability, not ready yet". ❌ Cancelled: "when a DCL breaks down post-trigger". 🔄 Updated: "when a new low replaces an invalidated one". **The rules that must "align" are not listed.** | It waits for the open or a strong bar: "trained to wait… Probably wants to see the open first", "the first big green candle will trigger the indicator to pick this". It labels a low only "through the window". In 2025: "waiting for the swing, the trend line break, the [Bressert] cross… and then the indicator will pick the low". | [CF docs: Daily Cycle Lows](https://camelfinance.gitbook.io/camel-finance-features-deep-dive/cf-cycle-trading-indicator/core-features/daily-cycle-lows.md), [The Lows Finally Confirmed !!, 2026-07-31, 07:21](https://youtu.be/bkf_ALW8W_A?t=441), [FOMC Shakeout / Trap & GO !!, 2026-09-16, 07:21](https://youtu.be/28wXrOWuVyA?t=441), [And No One Believes It..., 2026-10-15, 13:38](https://youtu.be/hhb_9awG53c?t=818), [The 4 Year Cycle is 95% Complete..., 2025-08-27, 02:31](https://youtu.be/9-JtVbT-4-o?t=151) |
| **Confirmation states (WCL)** | ❌ Cancelled WCL "Appears when price action breaks decisively below a previously confirmed WCL". No 🔄 is documented for WCLs. Whether a break means a close or a wick, and what "decisively" means, is not stated. | His own failure rule is a *close* below the confirmed low (13 Oct). | [CF docs: Weekly Cycle Lows](https://camelfinance.gitbook.io/camel-finance-features-deep-dive/cf-cycle-trading-indicator/core-features/weekly-cycle-lows.md), [It's Happening!, 2026-10-13, 06:18](https://youtu.be/qarjzOUTk8k?t=378) |
| **Pending (early warning)** | ⚠️ Pending DCL and ⚠️ Pending WCL alerts, "For fine-tuned assets only — an early heads-up that a DCL may be forming soon". The trigger is not stated. | Video wording: "the indicator is lining up a low probably within about a week". In 2025: "likely found an early weekly cycle low according to the indicator. So this will fully confirm once this thing can break to the upside." | [CF docs: Alerts](https://camelfinance.gitbook.io/camel-finance-features-deep-dive/cf-cycle-trading-indicator/additional-features/alerts.md), [Record Shorts, 2026-08-19, 05:38](https://youtu.be/O2e2PpZn6vA?t=338), [The 4 Year Cycle is 95% Complete..., 2025-08-27, 19:23](https://youtu.be/9-JtVbT-4-o?t=1163) |
| **DCL confluence grade** | Three components: "Breakout Confirmation – Detects clean breakouts aligned with cycle low timing"; "Momentum Signals – Assesses internal strength of the move"; "Supporting Indicators – Validates the price action with added context". Three grades: High = "strong probability", Mid = "moderate signal, proceed with caution", Low = "possible noise". **The component definitions, weights and cut-offs are not stated.** Only DCL confluence is documented. | The one spoken readout: "a close above the 10 SMA and a low picked. Okay, with medium confluence" (29 Jul). A user review on the sales page: "the recent updates that give low, med and high probability for the DCL". | [CF docs: Daily Cycle Lows](https://camelfinance.gitbook.io/camel-finance-features-deep-dive/cf-cycle-trading-indicator/core-features/daily-cycle-lows.md), [FOMC Arrives! ### Engine inputs and settings: the closest the docs come to a formula

The "Asset Count Adjustment" panel lists six settings and describes each one in words only. None has a default or a range stated. [CF docs: Asset Count Adjustment](https://camelfinance.gitbook.io/camel-finance-features-deep-dive/cf-cycle-trading-indicator/additional-features/asset-count-adjustment.md)
- **Cycle Length Adjustment Factor**: "Adjusts the average length of a cycle." This implies a base average cycle length per asset, scaled by a factor.
- **Fast EMA Adjustment Factor**: "Controls short-term sensitivity."
- **Slow EMA Adjustment Factor**: "Smooths the count over longer timeframes."
- **Overbought Filter (Uptrend Threshold)**: "Determines how quickly an asset is marked as entering an uptrend. Lower values = quicker uptrend detection."
- **Oversold Filter (Downtrend Threshold)**: "Determines how sensitive the system is to detecting downtrends. Higher values = slower, more conservative trend shifts."
- **Faster Confirmation (toggle)**: "Enables quicker DCL confirmation for aggressive traders."

Reading of these settings (an inference, not stated): the engine keeps an up/down trend state from a fast and a slow EMA of some series, gated by overbought/oversold thresholds, and combines it with an expected cycle length. The docs do **not** say:
- what the EMAs are applied to (price? an oscillator?);
- that DSS Bressert is involved. DSS Bressert appears only in a user review: "stacked with trendline breaks and DSS Bressert". [cftrading.co.uk sales page](https://www.cftrading.co.uk/)

**Templates.** "Finetuned Asset Selection" applies an asset's "pre-optimised cycle templates", "built using in-depth backtesting and refinement", to any chart (for example "Try applying the BTC or ETH template" to an altcoin). In the videos: "tens of thousands of different settings" are backtested per asset, and the "indicator is getting sharper" from that optimisation. [CF docs: Finetuned Asset Selection](https://camelfinance.gitbook.io/camel-finance-features-deep-dive/cf-cycle-trading-indicator/additional-features/finetuned-asset-selection.md), [AI Threat Arrives on Schedule, 2026-10-08, 01:25](https://youtu.be/xbRQulPGwkQ?t=85), [Record Shorts, 2026-08-19, 08:14](https://youtu.be/O2e2PpZn6vA?t=494)

**Implied by the videos, not stated as identical.** He offers the indicator as the automated alternative to his manual method: average the low-to-low spans over the whole history and apply a 10% tolerance. "Or of course you could just get the indicator. That's another way of doing it." **The docs never say "10%" or "tolerance".** [Answering Your Questions, 2026-08-18, 08:20](https://youtu.be/EKSjKkcIIqU?t=500), [Answering Your Questions, 2026-08-18, 09:22](https://youtu.be/EKSjKkcIIqU?t=562)

### What neither source states

These items are not stated anywhere:
- the detection formula;
- the swing or pivot definition and its width;
- the EMA lengths and their source series;
- the overbought/oversold values;
- the base cycle length per asset (the table's numbers are not published) and the window tolerance;
- the confirmation rules behind ✅, the trigger for "Pending", and what "Faster Confirmation" changes;
- close vs wick for ❌, and what "decisively" means;
- the confluence components (which breakout, which momentum measure, which "supporting indicators") and their weights;
- the MTR band;
- how a YCL is found;
- whether the 10 SMA gates a label;
- how inversions are labelled (seen in the videos, absent from the docs);
- any out-of-sample result.

DSS Bressert is a separate tool in the videos: "we have got a Bresset cross and we have got the indicator". [FOMC Arrives! Here's How I am Trading it, 2026-07-29, 02:03](https://youtu.be/nKpG-HTBRrQ?t=123), [FOMC Shakeout / Trap & GO !!, 2026-09-16, 07:51](https://youtu.be/28wXrOWuVyA?t=471)

- **Platform.** It is a TradingView script, sold through cftrading.co.uk (which moved from camelfinance.co.uk on 10 Aug 2026). From 1 Nov 2026 new customers must come "via their marketplace". [Gold at Critical Inflection Point !!, 2026-08-10, 00:32](https://youtu.be/MMnkrbLKZRc?t=32), [Here's What's Next !!, 2026-10-09, 01:37](https://youtu.be/9FrE_d7KaFc?t=97)
- **Prices.** **No price is spoken in any video.** The sales page (capture date not stated; its dashboard figures run to 16 Oct 2026) lists **$56/month, $560/year and $4,999 lifetime** in USD, all with a 14-day free trial ("2-week" elsewhere) and refunds within 7 days of a billing period. It claims "2,300+ active traders". In the videos he offered "50% off the lifetime package" for two weeks from 10 Aug. [cftrading.co.uk sales page](https://www.cftrading.co.uk/), [Gold at Critical Inflection Point !!, 2026-08-10, 12:15](https://youtu.be/MMnkrbLKZRc?t=735)
- **What else the subscription bundles**: the education library (cycle fundamentals, trendlines, hyperwave), a macro dashboard (FRED data), and a Discord community. It is separate from the Level 3 YouTube membership. [CF docs: Learn & Level-Up](https://camelfinance.gitbook.io/camel-finance-features-deep-dive/learn-and-level-up.md), [Time to LOCK IN !!, 2026-08-05, 03:08](https://youtu.be/jiaoYL45IrM?t=188)
- **Users report it working on 4h, 1h and 5-minute charts**, even though only 1D is "fine-tuned". These are testimonials, not tests. [cftrading.co.uk sales page](https://www.cftrading.co.uk/)

### What a clone would need to decide (neither source gives these)
1. **Swing detector**: the pivot definition (left/right bar counts), and whether a low may be labelled only inside the window. The videos say it labels only once "through the window".
2. **Cycle lengths per asset and degree**: use his spoken numbers (BTC 60 days / ~28–32 weeks / 4 years; S&P 40 days; gold 22–28 days, weeks 22–28; tolerance ±10%), or re-derive them from history. Also decide the "Cycle Length Adjustment Factor" default.
3. **The trend engine**: what series the fast and slow EMAs smooth, their lengths, and the overbought/oversold thresholds that set up/down state.
4. **The confirmation rule for ✅**: the candidates from his own speech are a close above SMA(10), a DSS Bressert (10,9,5) cross from below 20, a close above a down-trendline proxy, and a big green or thrust bar. Decide how many are required, and the "Faster Confirmation" variant.
5. **Cancel and update rules**: close vs wick, how much below counts as "decisively", and how the 🔄 replacement low is chosen (the lowest low after the cancel?).
6. **Pending (⚠️)**: when a provisional low becomes pending. For example, window open plus a new N-bar low, with no confirmation yet.
7. **The confluence score**: definitions for "breakout", "momentum" and "supporting indicators", and the High/Mid/Low cut-offs.
8. **Degree nesting**: how WCLs are chosen from DCLs (the lowest DCL in a weekly window? 3–5 dailies per weekly), how YCLs are chosen from WCLs, and when to label an inversion (a half-cycle low promoted to a DCL).
9. **FCLR geometry**: whether the box runs from the last confirmed low + mean length ± tolerance, and whether it updates only on a new confirmed DCL (as the docs say).
10. **Translation**: the top = highest high between two confirmed lows (or highest close), and the width of the MTR band around the midpoint.
11. **Timeframe**: daily bars only (as fine-tuned), or allow weekly-bar detection for WCLs.
12. **Pre-registration**: the clone must be tested as its own rule against controls and surrogates. The vendor's optimised numbers cannot validate it.

## Thesis timeline, July to October 2026

**Before the record (from the older videos).** On 27 Aug 2025 he expected the BTC top within 2–3 months, then a 50%+ fall to a 4-year low "around 70ishk" in late 2026, with the S&P and gold lows "right around the same time". On 7 May 2026 BTC was "hard rejected off of 82 k" and he expected a violent weekly-cycle low near 60K within ~3 weeks. That is the same yellow squiggle and roughly the same line that became the 83K gate in August. ## Dated calls (for later scoring)

All 358 rows, merged in order of video date: 325 from the five parts and 33 (marked ‡) from the later transcripts (2025-08-27, 2026-04-11 and 2026-05-07, and the dailies of 2026-08-29, 09-08 and 09-25). Within a date, rows keep their part order, with ‡ rows last. No row was literally duplicated, so none was dropped. Short citations from Part 2 have been expanded to the full form. Positions (the first rows of Part 1, e.g. "Long, entered") are mixed in with calls. Nothing has been scored.

## Candidate rules NOT in the pre-registered setup grader

These are merged and deduplicated across the five parts and ranked with the most testable first. Ranks 1–28 need only OHLC price data (some symbols, e.g. the BTC-proxy equities or DXY, may need fetching). Ranks 29–37 are computable but need data we do not have (most of it public). Ranks 38+ are retrospective or very small n. Terminal tools used: SMA, DSS Bressert, Parabolic SAR, ATR, cycles tooling, horizontal levels, RSI. "Cycles as a low detector" and "translation as direction" are dead in this repo. Where a candidate touches them, only the narrower claim is proposed. He proposes one control himself (2026-04-11): a trading variant should beat BTC buy-and-hold (~70% CAGR), or "would I be better off just holding Bitcoin?" [FREE VIDEO: 70% CAGR, Risk management & Mathematical example, 2026-04-11, 00:31](https://youtu.be/F7gm9z5G3lw?t=31)

1. **Close-based cycle stop vs intrabar stop.** Same entries (a long at a confirmed pivot low). Compare an exit on a daily *close* below the cycle low with an intrabar stop at low − k·ATR. This tests his claim that wicks and double-bottom sweeps are not invalidation. [Answering Your Questions, 2026-08-18, 01:06](https://youtu.be/EKSjKkcIIqU?t=66), [Answering Your Questions, 2026-08-18, 01:39](https://youtu.be/EKSjKkcIIqU?t=99), [It's Happening!, 2026-10-13, 06:18](https://youtu.be/qarjzOUTk8k?t=378)
2. **Confirmation-count gate (variants are his own dated wordings).** On the cycle's own timeframe, after a pivot low, count: (a) close > SMA(10); (b) DSS Bressert (10,9,5) %K crosses above %D from below 20 within 3 bars; (c) a thrust bar (range ≥ 1.5×ATR(14), close in the top 25%) or a bullish engulfing within 3 bars; (d) a close above the highest close of the last N bars (a proxy for the hand-drawn trendline); (e) a PSAR flip to long. Test (i) "any one of" (17 Sep / 2023), (ii) "all of (b)+(d)+pivot" (16 Sep / 13 Oct), and (iii) ≥3 of 5 ("two out of six" was too few). Run each as a filter on Cipher B Buy dots and stand-alone, exposure-matched. [Another FOMC Shakeout, 2026-07-28, 06:12](https://youtu.be/wyoC1nHivA8?t=372), [FOMC Shakeout / Trap & GO !!, 2026-09-16, 08:22](https://youtu.be/28wXrOWuVyA?t=502), [Major Opportunity for the Long term account, 2026-09-17, 17:52](https://youtu.be/ZlYblgqzJ2w?t=1072), [Does Sentiment Match a Bottom, 2026-08-25, 13:37](https://youtu.be/_freGHQfGEc?t=817), [HOW TO TRADE CYCLES, 2023-08-30, 10:33](https://youtu.be/XWTIrduvgrU?t=633)
3. **Trend-template box breakout (his 2022 momentum rule; fully mechanical).** Universe: stocks whose daily close is above SMA50, SMA150 and SMA200, with SMA150 > SMA200, SMA200 rising over the last 20 trading days, and SMA50 above both. Entry: a weekly close above the high of the latest box (a base, e.g. ≥4 weeks with range ≤ x%). Stop: below the box low (variant: halfway down the box), risk 1–2%. Re-enter on each new box breakout. Test his side-claim that later bases fail more often (hit rate by base number). Control: the same breakouts without the template, plus buy-and-hold. Needs an equity universe; OHLC + SMA only. [How to Find and Trade Momentum Stocks, 2022-08-23, 03:02](https://youtu.be/Tl1ObCPO9m0?t=182), [How to Find and Trade Momentum Stocks, 2022-08-23, 07:37](https://youtu.be/Tl1ObCPO9m0?t=457), [How to Find and Trade Momentum Stocks, 2022-08-23, 08:38](https://youtu.be/Tl1ObCPO9m0?t=518), [How to Find and Trade Momentum Stocks, 2022-08-23, 12:42](https://youtu.be/Tl1ObCPO9m0?t=762)
4. **DCL resolution as a "low is in" classifier (the 80/80 rule).** After a confirmed daily low near a 26-week low: "in" = a close above the prior daily-cycle high before any close below the DCL low; "not in" = a lower high, then a close below the DCL low. Score whether a lower low than the candidate prints within 26 weeks. He claims 80% either way. Run it across many assets (BTC alone gives n≈2). This is new-high-before-failure, not translation timing. [It's Happening!, 2026-10-13, 06:18](https://youtu.be/qarjzOUTk8k?t=378), [How I outperformed Bitcoin last cycle, 2026-10-14, 10:00](https://youtu.be/52ZlNcndcSE?t=600)
5. **Later entry: the breakout-retest higher low vs the first low.** (A) Long at a confirmed pivot low, stop under it. (B) Long only after a close above the last swing high, on the first higher-low pullback, stop under that higher low. Size both at 2% risk. Compare expectancy per unit of risk and hit rate. He claims B is better despite giving up ~500 gold points. [Time to LOCK IN !!, 2026-08-05, 06:41](https://youtu.be/jiaoYL45IrM?t=401), [More Upside Ahead, 2026-08-13, 08:28](https://youtu.be/ggtv4-O6Gdw?t=508), [Record Shorts, 2026-08-19, 05:38](https://youtu.be/O2e2PpZn6vA?t=338)
6. **Pyramid at successive confirmed DCLs, with one trailing line.** Add a unit (2% risk short-term, or a 5% clip long-term) at each new confirmed DCL that is a higher low, only if the previous unit is in profit. Move *all* stops to just under the newest DCL and exit everything on a close below it. Compare with a single entry and with exposure-matched buy-and-hold. [The Treasury Just Blinked, 2026-08-20, 09:07](https://youtu.be/_Gn7BpBvA44?t=547), [Major Opportunity for the Long term account, 2026-09-17, 09:26](https://youtu.be/ZlYblgqzJ2w?t=566), [Here's Why, 2026-09-18, 08:46](https://youtu.be/NUaGWQQTN5Y?t=526), [It's happening, 2026-09-12, 22:30](https://youtu.be/ZpYL6r2JzuY?t=1350)
7. **Higher-high-close regime switch.** Bull regime only after a weekly close above the most recent confirmed weekly swing-high pivot. Wicks do not count; optionally require 2 closes. Bear regime back on a lower high plus a close below the last confirmed DCL. Use it as a veto on daily long signals and compare with the grader's EMA/SMA phase. [Does Sentiment Match a Bottom, 2026-08-25, 13:07](https://youtu.be/_freGHQfGEc?t=787), [Crypto Bull Market Confirms Here, 2026-08-27, 02:39](https://youtu.be/RevmcdyF-5s?t=159), [We're So Back !!, 2026-09-22, 03:35](https://youtu.be/n3R4fE6denY?t=215)
8. **"First hurdle" veto in a down phase.** Reject daily long signals while the weekly phase is bear and no daily close has cleared the first swing high after the latest DCL. This is a sharper form of the grader's weekly veto. [It's a trap, 2026-07-27, 08:45](https://youtu.be/C3E_zU5a6c4?t=525), [Gold at Critical Inflection Point !!, 2026-08-10, 08:02](https://youtu.be/MMnkrbLKZRc?t=482)
9. **Timing window as a risk-placement filter (not a detector).** Is a stop under a pivot low found *inside* the asset's window less likely to be hit within N bars than an equal-distance stop under a pivot found outside it? Use his own numbers: BTC 54–66 days, S&P 36–44 trading days (half-cycle 18–24), gold 22–28 days. The window is the history-average ±10%. Control: surrogate cycles. [Answering Your Questions, 2026-08-18, 08:20](https://youtu.be/EKSjKkcIIqU?t=500), [HOW TO TRADE CYCLES, 2023-08-30, 02:33](https://youtu.be/XWTIrduvgrU?t=153), [Gold at Critical Inflection Point !!, 2026-08-10, 04:13](https://youtu.be/MMnkrbLKZRc?t=253), [It's Happening!, 2026-10-13, 01:01](https://youtu.be/qarjzOUTk8k?t=61)
10. **In-window base rate (70–80%; 80% in 2023).** With a fixed swing detector and his lengths, what share of lows falls within ±10% of the mean length? Must be compared with surrogates, because the detector sets the period. [HOW TO TRADE CYCLES, 2023-08-30, 05:16](https://youtu.be/XWTIrduvgrU?t=316), [Gold's Breakout is Extremely Important, 2026-08-06, 05:44](https://youtu.be/iZYEi03ZddY?t=344), [The Next Move Will Shock Investors, 2026-09-10, 15:00](https://youtu.be/cp7MHZZBFW8?t=900)
11. **Late-cycle no-breakout rule (BTC).** With the DCL anchor fixed: if by day ≥ 45–50 of 60 there has been no close above the cycle high, measure the forward return to day 60, and the frequency of a new high in the last quarter of the cycle compared with any 15-day span. His claims: tops come on days 40–50, and "timing-based consolidations" are rare (two or three in four years). [It's Happening Again !! (Asset Cycles Aligning), 2026-09-07, 04:11](https://youtu.be/OqzFvsbmfGE?t=251), [Simulation Confirmed, Narrative Arrives, 2026-09-14, 14:42](https://youtu.be/LK5YLwkwCiI?t=882)
12. **Equity half-cycle-low trigger.** On S&P/US100 in trading days from the last DCL: on days 18–24, a green close above the prior high with range ≥ 1×ATR(14) = the half-cycle low. Go long with the stop under it. Compare with random days. [The Most Important Level In Finance, 2026-08-26, 07:12](https://youtu.be/c46MwOOnips?t=432), [How I outperformed Bitcoin last cycle, 2026-10-14, 13:07](https://youtu.be/52ZlNcndcSE?t=787), [This Changes Things, 2026-10-17, 16:28](https://youtu.be/l4d4MOUG1Gg?t=988)
13. **Back-test of a fresh cycle low on bars 5–10 (Andrew, 2024; not Camel's).** After a confirmed DCL or WCL (a pivot plus a close above SMA10), test whether price returns to within k·ATR of the low on bars 5–10, compared with random bars. Also compare buying that back-test, with the stop under the low, against buying the confirmation. Computable with OHLC + ATR. [Cycle Trading with Andrew (@GoldnGuitars), 2024-08-10, 02:06](https://youtu.be/KSum66r_w8s?t=126), [Cycle Trading with Andrew (@GoldnGuitars), 2024-08-10, 25:03](https://youtu.be/KSum66r_w8s?t=1503)
14. **Monthly 4-year-low confirmation.** A monthly close > SMA(10 months) plus a monthly DSS Bressert bullish cross, with ≥2 weekly closes above the weekly SMA(10). Invalidation = the next weekly close back below. [Does Sentiment Match a Bottom, 2026-08-25, 14:07](https://youtu.be/_freGHQfGEc?t=847), [Does Sentiment Match a Bottom, 2026-08-25, 15:08](https://youtu.be/_freGHQfGEc?t=908)
15. **Weekly pivot confirmation (Dow).** After a weekly swing low, a weekly close above the pivot week's high confirms the weekly low. Measure the forward return of adding on that close. [This Changes Things, 2026-10-17, 18:02](https://youtu.be/l4d4MOUG1Gg?t=1082)
16. **Failed weekly cycle means the next low is lower.** Weekly cycle = the span between confirmed weekly pivots; failed = a weekly close below the cycle's starting low. Test only whether the next weekly low prints below the failed cycle's low, against surrogates. It is close to dead ground, so its priority is low. [How I outperformed Bitcoin last cycle, 2026-10-14, 02:37](https://youtu.be/52ZlNcndcSE?t=157), [The Next Leg Higher has Started!, 2026-10-03, 20:08](https://youtu.be/IYfTEZtA2f8?t=1208)
17. **Weekly DSS "fake cross, then higher low" divergence.** Weekly DSS Bressert crosses up and uncrosses within ≤2 bars, then makes a higher low against a lower price low. Buy the next weekly bullish cross. This is a weekly variant of the grader's divergence layer. [The Lows Finally Confirmed !!, 2026-07-31, 06:19](https://youtu.be/bkf_ALW8W_A?t=379), [The Lows Finally Confirmed !!, 2026-07-31, 06:49](https://youtu.be/bkf_ALW8W_A?t=409)
18. **BTC proxies lag BTC's low.** After BTC's confirmed weekly cycle low, do COIN, MSTR, RIOT, MARA, CLSK, BITF, HUT, CIFR, BTBT, IREN, WULF and HIVE make a lower low within N weeks? And does buying their later low beat buying BTC at its low over 12–24 months at 2% risk? Episodes: 2018–19 and 2022–23. Controls: the same names bought at BTC's low and at random dates. [How I outperformed Bitcoin last cycle, 2026-10-14, 03:40](https://youtu.be/52ZlNcndcSE?t=220), [How I outperformed Bitcoin last cycle, 2026-10-14, 08:27](https://youtu.be/52ZlNcndcSE?t=507)
19. **Leader/laggard tests.** (a) ETH wick-fill: when ETH closes above its last swing high and BTC has not, "leading" = a close above the breakout candle's high within 5 days. Record whether BTC follows. (b) Semis canary: SMH/SOX lower high plus a close below its last DCL while SPX/NDX are above theirs, then measure the forward NDX/SPX return. [It's happening, 2026-09-12, 04:22](https://youtu.be/ZpYL6r2JzuY?t=262), [Crypto Bull Market Confirms Here, 2026-08-27, 08:01](https://youtu.be/RevmcdyF-5s?t=481), [The Wait is Almost Over, 2026-08-17, 04:48](https://youtu.be/Q_U-MbBmLdI?t=288)
20. **Gold breakout needs the 200-day MA.** A gold breakout above the N-day high counts only if the close is also above SMA(200). Compare follow-through with and without the condition. [More Upside Ahead, 2026-08-13, 04:14](https://youtu.be/ggtv4-O6Gdw?t=254)
21. **Retracement claims.** (a) After a straight-line push (≥8 of 10 bars up), expect a ~50% retracement before continuation. (b) After a parabolic advance breaks (a 20-bar log slope ≥ 2× the prior 60-bar slope, then a close below EMA(20)), the median retrace is ~70% of the leg. [The Most Important Level In Finance, 2026-08-26, 08:14](https://youtu.be/c46MwOOnips?t=494), [We've Reached a Major Inflection Point, 2026-09-30, 02:05](https://youtu.be/O2PXnNlV3gA?t=125)
22. **Gap back-fill after a thrust.** After ≥2 consecutive gap-ups in the Dow off a DCL, expect ≥2 red days but no close below the DCL before new highs. [We are printing again, 2026-08-07, 02:37](https://youtu.be/ORSNkP6I4jI?t=157)
23. **BTC long-period MAs.** (a) Above the 200-week SMA 92% of weeks; does a weekly close at or below it mark a forward-return extreme? (b) After a 50/200 golden cross, a weekly close above the 50-week SMA with a higher high ends the bear, while rejection means lower lows. (c) The first weekly close above the 50-week after ≥40 weeks below: hit rate of reaching the 100-week MA. n is tiny for all three. [It's Make or Break, 2026-08-03, 04:57](https://youtu.be/jweSBXongzE?t=297), [We're So Back, 2026-09-09, 07:14](https://youtu.be/O_zc6fm1I4g?t=434), [Feeling FOMO? Watch This!, 2026-09-21, 09:17](https://youtu.be/853X_T8T3Ow?t=557)
24. **Stage-2 entry on the 30-week MA (stage analysis: his 2022 framework, quoted again on 2026-09-25).** Long on the first weekly close above a rising 30-week SMA after a base in which price crossed the MA at least twice (his "oscillation" condition). Exit on a weekly close below a falling 30-week SMA. Compare with and without the oscillation condition. Computable. [The Blow Off Top Continues, 2026-09-25, 02:35](https://youtu.be/luWzbwZ02mg?t=155), [The Blow Off Top Continues, 2026-09-25, 04:14](https://youtu.be/luWzbwZ02mg?t=254), [How to Find and Trade Momentum Stocks, 2022-08-23, 00:30](https://youtu.be/Tl1ObCPO9m0?t=30)
25. **S&P Q4 seasonality conditional.** YTD +10–20% at the 30 Sep close implies Q4 > 0 in 85% of years and +5.3% on average. Control: the unconditional Q4. [A Bullish Q4 is Loading !!, 2026-10-02, 03:47](https://youtu.be/MG8WI9O0Eyk?t=227)
26. **Calendar effects.** (a) "Destruction Monday, turnaround Tuesday": Tuesday up-closes after Monday down-closes vs the base rate. (b) SPY overnight vs intraday returns. [Bitcoin Bulls Got Real Quiet All of a Sudden..., 2026-09-01, 00:31](https://youtu.be/n3XxAlp79oM?t=31), [We're So Back !!, 2026-09-22, 07:48](https://youtu.be/n3R4fE6denY?t=468)

Use a fixed long-period pivot detector. [It's starting, 2026-08-01, 18:27](https://youtu.be/L3QWk203NR4?t=1107), [Another FOMC Shakeout, 2026-07-28, 15:51](https://youtu.be/wyoC1nHivA8?t=951), [This Changes Things, 2026-10-17, 23:24](https://youtu.be/l4d4MOUG1Gg?t=1404)
28. **Left translation plus failure as a stop-and-reverse on the long book.** If a daily cycle's high prints before its midpoint (or by day 30 of 60) and price then closes below the cycle's starting low, exit the longs and go short, stop above the cycle high. Test only whether it cuts drawdown compared with the stop alone, since translation-as-direction is dead. [Another FOMC Shakeout, 2026-07-28, 07:43](https://youtu.be/wyoC1nHivA8?t=463), [Crypto Bull Market Confirms Here, 2026-08-27, 07:30](https://youtu.be/RevmcdyF-5s?t=450), [HOW TO TRADE CYCLES, 2023-08-30, 12:35](https://youtu.be/XWTIrduvgrU?t=755)
29. **Regime gate as an exit (needs data we do not have: FRED HY OAS BAMLH0A0HYM2, T10Y2Y, T10Y3M, which are public but not in repo snapshots).** Go flat on equities when *all* of these hold: (a) the SPX has a failed daily cycle; (b) a trendline-break proxy (a close below a rising 50-day regression line); (c) HY OAS closes above its trailing 26-week high, or the 10y−3m weekly change is > 2σ of its 52-week changes. Variant (17 Oct): flat by mid-week if (c) holds and the SPX closed lower on every day of the prior week. A poke that closes back inside within a week is ignored. Benchmark: close > SMA(200). [The Year End Everything Rally, 2026-09-26, 14:34](https://youtu.be/_gS-hSL_vOI?t=874), [The Next Leg Higher has Started!, 2026-10-03, 13:57](https://youtu.be/IYfTEZtA2f8?t=837), [This Changes Things, 2026-10-17, 14:20](https://youtu.be/l4d4MOUG1Gg?t=860), [It's starting, 2026-08-01, 13:22](https://youtu.be/L3QWk203NR4?t=802)

31. **Credit vs equity vol divergence leads to a VIX spike.** HY OAS rising while VIX falls (z-score gap above a threshold), then a VIX spike or SPX drawdown within N weeks vs the unconditional rate. Needs data we do not have (HY OAS, VIX history). [We've Reached a Major Inflection Point, 2026-09-30, 05:14](https://youtu.be/O2PXnNlV3gA?t=314)

Compare its lead time with "three consecutive higher-high prints". Needs FRED CPI. Needs a volume-profile computation. [A Massive Week Ahead, 2026-08-31, 03:48](https://youtu.be/-HDKylerUWo?t=228), [A Massive Week Ahead, 2026-08-31, 04:19](https://youtu.be/-HDKylerUWo?t=259)

38. **Halving 60% rule**: a significant decline soon after 60% of the halving interval has elapsed. n = 3–4, so retrospective risk is high. [We're So Back, 2026-09-09, 11:33](https://youtu.be/O_zc6fm1I4g?t=693)
39. **Sizing rules as portfolio simulation**: fixed 2% vs cutting risk after losses; a 6% sector cap; long-term "size to zero" with a 6–10% drawdown cap; sizing to the worst-case low (TLT 20% × ~10% = 2%). These are construction rules, not an edge, and can be tested only over a given trade list. ## Not usable / vague

- **The yellow squiggle.** In his own words it is "not designed to predict time or price". It gets redrawn on air ("this is way too long"), dragged higher (silver, 1 Sep) and shifted ~2 months right (gold, 29 Sep). Its turn date slid from ~29 Aug to "a few weeks". Two squiggles (short-term and long-term) are kept "in play" at once. Only the invalidation levels hung on it can be scored. [It's starting, 2026-08-01, 16:56](https://youtu.be/L3QWk203NR4?t=1016), [Bitcoin Bulls Got Real Quiet All of a Sudden..., 2026-09-01, 12:10](https://youtu.be/n3XxAlp79oM?t=730), [Huge Announcement Coming Today, 2026-09-29, 02:05](https://youtu.be/0zlHZQzW28Q?t=125), [And No One Believes It..., 2026-10-15, 12:38](https://youtu.be/hhb_9awG53c?t=758)
- **"All dips are for buying, higher low or lower low"** (BTC, gold, MSTR, metals into the year-end window). The buy happens whatever the price, so it cannot be falsified. [Bitcoin - Dip or Lockout Rally ??, 2026-09-23, 06:45](https://youtu.be/l8tdmwnJxSk?t=405), [The Year End Everything Rally, 2026-09-26, 04:15](https://youtu.be/_gS-hSL_vOI?t=255)
- **Relabelling inversions, failed cycles and expanded flats.** The count is reassessed "with the new data presented". The expanded flat keeps the bear path alive after its invalidation fired. The only falsifiable form is the 80% rule. [It's Started, 2026-08-04, 11:01](https://youtu.be/JoRNW_4jCVc?t=661), [It's Looking Good!, 2026-10-06, 06:25](https://youtu.be/un3YVNZSEVQ?t=385), [This Changes Things, 2026-10-17, 03:39](https://youtu.be/l4d4MOUG1Gg?t=219)
- **Elliott Wave counts** (mostly borrowed from Green Star Trading and Henrik): three paths cover up, down and up-then-down. They are relabelled as price arrives, and the BTC count was "shifted around a few times". [Bitcoin has 3 Possible Paths from here, 2026-09-05, 02:41](https://youtu.be/MK47H-8RTBo?t=161), [Did Gold Just Show What's Next for Bitcoin?, 2026-09-28, 06:31](https://youtu.be/JBHwqCwJma8?t=391)
- **Fractal overlays and analogues.** C-19 S&P month 48; the 2018 BTC 6K floor at 60K; the DAX blow-off; Nike→BTC; dot-com→SOX; BTC vs gold cup-and-handle (to 2.8M); NVDA→BTC (rejected by him); the 2018 midterm 10y; George Baer's "Egg of Columbus" ("absolutely crackhead TA", his words). All retrospective. He calls fractals "the crayons of TA". [Gold at Critical Inflection Point !!, 2026-08-10, 06:23](https://youtu.be/MMnkrbLKZRc?t=383), [New Lows Coming For Crypto, 2026-08-11, 02:03](https://youtu.be/UcAX4VpRTfg?t=123), [Feeling FOMO? Watch This!, 2026-09-21, 10:19](https://youtu.be/853X_T8T3Ow?t=619), [Bitcoin - Dip or Lockout Rally ??, 2026-09-23, 12:20](https://youtu.be/l8tdmwnJxSk?t=740)
- **Hand-drawn lines with no number.** The red descending resistance lines, the "third angle", the 10y "blue range", the TLT "box", the stocks' "red legacy trendline", gold's "bull market breakout level", pitchfork median lines (his and Cobra's), and the oil "highs". [What is Going on ??, 2026-09-15, 01:20](https://youtu.be/wqa0EcxKOYY?t=80), [We're So Back !!, 2026-09-22, 08:48](https://youtu.be/n3R4fE6denY?t=528), [We've Reached a Major Inflection Point, 2026-09-30, 04:09](https://youtu.be/O2PXnNlV3gA?t=249), [Rejected at Resistance, 2026-08-14, 08:17](https://youtu.be/xXRr9E2Caqs?t=497)
- **"The most important level in finance" in October.** The number is never spoken. It is 83K only from August context. [AI Threat Arrives on Schedule, 2026-10-08, 04:44](https://youtu.be/xbRQulPGwkQ?t=284)
- **Sentiment from his own X feed, comments, polls and "the camel crew".** These are not reproducible. He himself says the "For You" feed is "all noise and no signal", and calls his own polls "not really reliable at all". [The Funniest Analysis I've Ever Seen!, 2026-09-02, 07:00](https://youtu.be/DPhLPRVnliM?t=420), [A Bullish Q4 is Loading !!, 2026-10-02, 04:51](https://youtu.be/MG8WI9O0Eyk?t=291), [Gold's Breakout is Extremely Important, 2026-08-06, 07:22](https://youtu.be/iZYEi03ZddY?t=442)

- **The CF indicator's labels and its dashboard claims.** It is closed source, so it can only be approximated (window + pivot). The vendor's backtests are optimised over "millions" of settings with no out-of-sample split described. [AI Threat Arrives on Schedule, 2026-10-08, 01:25](https://youtu.be/xbRQulPGwkQ?t=85)
- **Long-range targets**: gold 15K–42K, silver 1,100 / "10x", BTC 300K–2.8M, S&P 8K, NASDAQ 37K–40K. He calls them "worthless guess[es]". The gold "15K" passage is garbled in the transcript. [This Changes Things, 2026-10-17, 19:09](https://youtu.be/l4d4MOUG1Gg?t=1149), [WE Are Gonna Make It !!, 2026-09-04, 10:31](https://youtu.be/f8JJ3myLgR8?t=631)
- **The "Day 837 / 900-day golden pocket"**: the anchor is unstated. **The MSTR "22 bucks"** squiggle level is ASR-uncertain. **The VIX "close the week somewhere up here"**: no level. [Gold's Breakout is Extremely Important, 2026-08-06, 03:39](https://youtu.be/iZYEi03ZddY?t=219), [Answering Your Questions, 2026-08-18, 04:48](https://youtu.be/EKSjKkcIIqU?t=288), [This Changes Things, 2026-10-17, 18:35](https://youtu.be/l4d4MOUG1Gg?t=1115)
- **The 2024 "principles"** (admit you don't know, one day at a time, abandon the need to be right) are habits, not rules. **Hyperwave phase targets (2025)** and **Andrew's 17-year top cycle, forks and ratio charts (2024)** are retrospective. [Principles for staying ahead in investing, 2024-01-30, 06:37](https://youtu.be/4wc4EOj6KOo?t=397), [The 4 Year Cycle is 95% Complete..., 2025-08-27, 13:47](https://youtu.be/9-JtVbT-4-o?t=827), [Cycle Trading with Andrew (@GoldnGuitars), 2024-08-10, 37:25](https://youtu.be/KSum66r_w8s?t=2245)
- **Members-only levels**: every BTC, US100, semis and Dow entry and stop goes to Level 3 only, so the positions cannot be scored at entry/stop level. Macro data-distrust claims ("a lie", shelter lag) are opinion. ## Ledger corrections

Corrections to `docs/CAMEL_TRACK_RECORD.md` (and `docs/CAMEL_CYCLE_LIVE_READ.md`) found by the parts. Added at the end: disagreements between the parts, and corrections from this consolidation.

1. **The self-audit is periodic.** 25 Jul, 1 Aug and 8 Aug are the same "weekend deep dive… back through the base case" format as 15 Aug. (§8 asked.) "Answering Your Questions" was published 18 Aug and recorded 14 Aug. Disagreements between the parts:
- **Which oil invalidation was "first".** Part 3 says 11 Sep, Part 4 says 23 Sep ("first time set") and Part 5 says 13 Oct ("for the first time in this record"). The transcripts have all three. 11 Sep is the earliest.
- **The gold long.** Part 3 has it stopped by 1–4 Sep. Part 5's tracker summarises the August state as "declared the cycle failed and expected to be stopped" and says the long is never mentioned in October. Both are consistent with the transcripts: the stop was run in early September, and October is silent.
- **Part 4 calls 22 Sep a Monday.** It was a Tuesday. The point it makes still holds: the break was accepted before any weekly close.
- **Part 5 says "no public videos for 10-16".** The manifest lists a public 2026-10-16 video ("This Week Saw Panic - is it over?") that was **not obtained**. The same applies to 29 Aug, 8 Sep and 25 Sep.

Corrections from this consolidation:
- **The arrow colours at 18 Aug 08:20 are his manual ones.** Part 2 attributes them to the indicator; in context (07:50–08:20) he is placing TradingView arrows by hand while teaching the manual count. The vendor docs do give the indicator matching default colours (DCL 🟢, WCL 🔵, YCL gold/yellow recommended), all user-set. So the scheme is the same, but that 18 Aug passage is not about the indicator. [Answering Your Questions, 2026-08-18, 07:50](https://youtu.be/EKSjKkcIIqU?t=470), [CF docs: Alerts](https://camelfinance.gitbook.io/camel-finance-features-deep-dive/cf-cycle-trading-indicator/additional-features/alerts.md), [CF docs: Yearly Cycle Lows](https://camelfinance.gitbook.io/camel-finance-features-deep-dive/cf-cycle-trading-indicator/core-features/yearly-cycle-lows.md)
- **The 2023 tutorial gives different cycle numbers.** BTC weekly is 24–28 weeks (vs 28–32 in 2026), the S&P weekly 22–26 weeks, and the in-window rate 80%. Its confirmation rule is "any of" swing low, trendline break or 10-MA cross. He credits the system to Bob Loukas, who he believes got it from Charles Nenner.
